# ai_decision.py
# System decyzyjny AI dla Monster Engine 5.0

import random
from domains import get_effective_relationship, can_attack
from combat import calculate_hit_chance

def can_unit_move(unit):
    """Sprawdza czy jednostka może się ruszać (brak ROOT/STUN)."""
    for status in unit.get("statuses", []):
        if status["type"] in ["ROOT", "STUN"]:
            return False
    return True

# === STAŁE ===
SKIP_CHANCE = 0.20  # 20% szans na pominięcie każdego poziomu drzewka


def get_distance(pos_a, pos_b):
    """Zwraca odległość Czebyszewa między dwoma pozycjami."""
    return max(abs(pos_a[0] - pos_b[0]), abs(pos_a[1] - pos_b[1]))


def get_units_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val):
    """Zwraca jednostki w zasięgu range_val od unit."""
    result = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        dist = get_distance(unit["position"], other["position"])
        if dist <= range_val:
            result.append(other)
    return result


def get_enemies_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val):
    """Zwraca wrogów w zasięgu."""
    in_range = get_units_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val)
    enemies = []
    for other in in_range:
        rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
        if rel == "ENEMY":
            enemies.append(other)
    return enemies


def get_neutrals_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val):
    """Zwraca neutralnych w zasięgu."""
    in_range = get_units_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val)
    neutrals = []
    for other in in_range:
        rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
        if rel == "NEUTRAL":
            neutrals.append(other)
    return neutrals


def get_allies_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val):
    """Zwraca sojuszników w zasięgu."""
    in_range = get_units_in_range(unit, all_units, range_val)
    allies = []
    for other in in_range:
        rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
        if rel == "ALLY":
            allies.append(other)
    return allies


def get_available_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca listę dostępnych zdolności (cooldown = 0, brak blokujących statusów)."""
    has_stun = any(s["type"] == "STUN" for s in unit.get("statuses", []))
    has_silence = any(s["type"] == "SILENCE" for s in unit.get("statuses", []))
    has_disarm = any(s["type"] == "DISARM" for s in unit.get("statuses", []))
    
    available = []
    for slot in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]:
        if unit["cooldowns"].get(slot, 0) > 0:
            continue
        if has_stun:
            continue
        if slot in ["C", "D"] and has_silence:
            continue
        if slot in ["A", "B"] and has_disarm:
            continue
        ability = unit["abilities"][slot]
        
        # EMPOWERED: wymaga 3 stacków SETUP (SACRIFICE nie wymaga)
        if ability.get("empowered") and not ability.get("sacrifice") and unit.get("setup_stacks", 0) < 3:
            continue
        
        # SETUP: pomijaj jeśli stacks już na max
        if ability.get("type") == "SETUP" and unit.get("setup_stacks", 0) >= 3:
            continue
        
        available.append({
            "slot": slot,
            "ability": ability
        })
    return available


def get_offensive_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne zdolności ofensywne (DD/CC/DOT/AOE offensive + RES debuffy + DISPEL)."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    offensive = []
    for item in available:
        ability_type = item["ability"].get("type", "")
        
        # EMPOWERED: wymaga 3 stacków
        if item["ability"].get("empowered") and unit.get("setup_stacks", 0) < 3:
            continue

        # DD/CC/DOT/CHARM/FEAR/DRAINLIFE/DISPEL
        if ability_type in ["CON_DMG", "DOT", "STUN", "ROOT", "SILENCE", "DISARM", "CHARM", "FEAR", "DRAINLIFE", "DRAINLIFE_DOT", "DISPEL"]:
            offensive.append(item)
            continue

        # AOE — tylko ofensywne subtypy
        if ability_type == "AOE":
            aoe_type = item["ability"].get("aoe_type", "DD")
            if aoe_type in ["DD", "DOT", "CHARM", "FEAR", "DRAINLIFE"]:
                offensive.append(item)
            elif isinstance(aoe_type, str) and aoe_type.startswith("RES_") and ("VULN" in aoe_type or "WEAKEN" in aoe_type):
                offensive.append(item)
            continue

        # RES debuffy (wszystkie RES_* z VULN lub WEAKEN_ATTACK)
        if isinstance(ability_type, str) and ability_type.startswith("RES_"):
            if ("VULN" in ability_type) or ("WEAKEN_ATTACK" in ability_type):
                offensive.append(item)
                continue

    return offensive


def get_healing_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne zdolności leczące (HEAL + TEMP_HP + AOE HEAL/HOT/TEMP_HP)."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    healing = []
    for item in available:
        if item["ability"].get("type") in ["HEAL", "TEMP_HP"]:
            healing.append(item)
        elif item["ability"].get("type") == "AOE" and item["ability"].get("aoe_type") in ["HEAL", "HOT", "TEMP_HP"]:
            healing.append(item)
    return healing


def get_cleanse_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne zdolności CLEANSE (single + AOE)."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    result = []
    for item in available:
        if item["ability"].get("type") == "CLEANSE":
            result.append(item)
        elif item["ability"].get("type") == "AOE" and item["ability"].get("aoe_type") == "CLEANSE":
            result.append(item)
    return result


def get_dispel_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne zdolności DISPEL (single + AOE)."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    result = []
    for item in available:
        if item["ability"].get("type") == "DISPEL":
            result.append(item)
        elif item["ability"].get("type") == "AOE" and item["ability"].get("aoe_type") == "DISPEL":
            result.append(item)
    return result


def has_negative_effects(unit):
    """Sprawdza czy jednostka ma negatywne efekty (do CLEANSE)."""
    neg_statuses = ["STUN", "ROOT", "SILENCE", "DISARM", "CHARMED", "FEARED", "TAUNTED"]
    for s in unit.get("statuses", []):
        if s["type"] in neg_statuses:
            return True
    for m in unit.get("res_modifiers", []):
        if m.get("mode") in ["VULN", "WEAKEN_ATTACK"]:
            return True
    for e in unit.get("ot_effects", []):
        if e.get("type") in ["DOT", "DRAIN_DOT"]:
            return True
    return False


def has_positive_effects(unit):
    """Sprawdza czy jednostka ma pozytywne efekty (do DISPEL)."""
    for m in unit.get("res_modifiers", []):
        if m.get("mode") in ["SHIELD", "PIERCE"] or m.get("type") == "RES_TEMP":
            return True
    for e in unit.get("ot_effects", []):
        if e.get("type") == "HOT":
            return True
    if unit.get("pending_heals"):
        return True
    if unit.get("reflect_shields"):
        return True
    return False


def get_res_buff_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne RES buffy (SHIELD/PIERCE + AOE RES buff)."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    buffs = []
    for item in available:
        t = item["ability"].get("type", "")
        if isinstance(t, str) and t.startswith("RES_"):
            if ("SHIELD" in t) or ("PIERCE" in t):
                buffs.append(item)
        elif t == "AOE":
            aoe_t = item["ability"].get("aoe_type", "")
            if isinstance(aoe_t, str) and aoe_t.startswith("RES_") and ("SHIELD" in aoe_t or "PIERCE" in aoe_t):
                buffs.append(item)
    return buffs


def get_res_temp_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne zdolności RES_TEMP (buff odporności)."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    buffs = []
    for item in available:
        if item["ability"].get("type") == "RES_TEMP":
            buffs.append(item)
    return buffs


def get_reflect_abilities(unit):
    """Zwraca dostępne zdolności REFLECT."""
    available = get_available_abilities(unit)
    result = []
    for item in available:
        if item["ability"].get("type") == "REFLECT":
            result.append(item)
    return result


def target_has_effect(target, ability_name):
    """
    Sprawdza czy cel już ma aktywny efekt o danej nazwie zdolności.
    Sprawdza: temp_hp_effects, ot_effects, res_modifiers, reflect_shields, statuses.
    """
    for e in target.get("temp_hp_effects", []):
        if e.get("ability_name") == ability_name:
            return True
    for e in target.get("ot_effects", []):
        if e.get("ability_name") == ability_name:
            return True
    for e in target.get("res_modifiers", []):
        if e.get("ability_name") == ability_name:
            return True
    for e in target.get("reflect_shields", []):
        if e.get("ability_name") == ability_name:
            return True
    for e in target.get("statuses", []):
        if e.get("ability_name") == ability_name:
            return True
    return False


def ability_can_reach(ability, distance):
    """Sprawdza czy zdolność może dosięgnąć celu w danej odległości."""
    ability_range = ability.get("range", 0)
    
    # AOE: range + effect_range = total reach
    if ability.get("type") == "AOE":
        eff_range = int(ability.get("effect_range", 0))
        base_range = int(ability_range) if isinstance(ability_range, (int, float)) else 0
        return distance <= base_range + eff_range
    
    if ability_range == 1:
        return distance <= 1
    elif ability_range == 0:
        return distance == 0
    else:
        return distance <= ability_range


def get_best_offensive_ability(unit, target, game_map=None):
    """
    Wybiera najlepszą zdolność ofensywną przeciwko celowi.
    Wybiera zdolność z najwyższą szansą trafienia.
    Pomija zdolności z 0% szansą.
    """
    distance = get_distance(unit["position"], target["position"])
    offensive = get_offensive_abilities(unit)
    
    # Pobierz teren
    terrain_id = None
    if game_map:
        x, y = unit["position"]
        if 0 <= y < len(game_map) and 0 <= x < len(game_map[0]):
            terrain_id = game_map[y][x]
    
    # Filtruj po zasięgu i oblicz szanse
    candidates = []
    for item in offensive:
        if not ability_can_reach(item["ability"], distance):
            continue
        
        # DISPEL: pomijaj jeśli cel nie ma pozytywnych efektów
        ab_type = item["ability"].get("type", "")
        if ab_type == "DISPEL" and not has_positive_effects(target):
            continue
        if ab_type == "AOE" and item["ability"].get("aoe_type") == "DISPEL" and not has_positive_effects(target):
            continue
        
        # Duplikat: pomijaj jeśli cel już ma efekt tej zdolności
        ab_name = item["ability"].get("name", "")
        if ab_name and target_has_effect(target, ab_name):
            continue
        
        # Oblicz szansę trafienia "na sucho"
        hit_chance = calculate_hit_chance(unit, target, item["slot"], terrain_id)
        
        # Pomijaj zdolności z 0% szansą
        if hit_chance <= 0:
            continue
        
        candidates.append({
            "slot": item["slot"],
            "ability": item["ability"],
            "hit_chance": hit_chance
        })
    
    if not candidates:
        return None
    
    # Sortuj po szansie trafienia (najwyższa pierwsza)
    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x["hit_chance"], reverse=True)
    
    return candidates[0]


def get_direction_towards(from_pos, to_pos):
    """Zwraca kierunek ruchu od from_pos w stronę to_pos."""
    dx = to_pos[0] - from_pos[0]
    dy = to_pos[1] - from_pos[1]
    
    if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
        return None
    
    dx = max(-1, min(1, dx))
    dy = max(-1, min(1, dy))
    
    direction_map = {
        (0, 1): "N", (1, 1): "NE", (1, 0): "E", (1, -1): "SE",
        (0, -1): "S", (-1, -1): "SW", (-1, 0): "W", (-1, 1): "NW"
    }
    
    return direction_map.get((dx, dy))


def get_direction_away(from_pos, to_pos, game_map):
    """Zwraca kierunek ucieczki od to_pos."""
    dx = from_pos[0] - to_pos[0]
    dy = from_pos[1] - to_pos[1]
    
    # Normalizuj do -1, 0, 1
    dx = max(-1, min(1, dx))
    dy = max(-1, min(1, dy))
    
    # Jeśli jesteśmy w tym samym miejscu, losowy
    if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
        return get_random_direction()
    
    # Mapuj na kierunek
    direction_map = {
        (0, 1): "N", (1, 1): "NE", (1, 0): "E", (1, -1): "SE",
        (0, -1): "S", (-1, -1): "SW", (-1, 0): "W", (-1, 1): "NW"
    }
    
    direction = direction_map.get((dx, dy))
    
    # Sprawdź czy ruch jest legalny (w granicach mapy)
    if direction:
        # Symuluj
        delta = direction_to_delta(direction)
        nx, ny = from_pos[0] + delta[0], from_pos[1] + delta[1]
        if 0 <= nx < len(game_map[0]) and 0 <= ny < len(game_map):
            return direction
    
    # Jeśli zablokowany, spróbuj losowy (desperacja)
    return get_random_direction()
    dx = to_pos[0] - from_pos[0]
    dy = to_pos[1] - from_pos[1]
    
    # Normalizuj do -1, 0, 1
    dx = max(-1, min(1, dx))
    dy = max(-1, min(1, dy))
    
    direction_map = {
        (0, 1): "N",
        (1, 1): "NE",
        (1, 0): "E",
        (1, -1): "SE",
        (0, -1): "S",
        (-1, -1): "SW",
        (-1, 0): "W",
        (-1, 1): "NW",
        (0, 0): None
    }
    
    return direction_map.get((dx, dy))


def get_random_direction():
    """Zwraca losowy kierunek ruchu."""
    directions = ["N", "NE", "E", "SE", "S", "SW", "W", "NW"]
    return random.choice(directions)


def should_skip():
    """Sprawdza czy pominąć aktualny poziom drzewka (20% szans)."""
    return random.random() < SKIP_CHANCE


def decide_actions(unit, all_units, game_map):
    """
    Główna funkcja decyzyjna.
    
    Zwraca listę do 3 akcji:
    [
        {"type": "MOVE", "direction": "N"},
        {"type": "ABILITY", "slot": "A", "target": "Nazwa"},
        {"type": "ABILITY", "slot": "B", "target": "Nazwa"}
    ]
    """
    actions = []
    max_actions = 3
    
    # Kopia cooldownów do śledzenia użycia w tej turze
    used_abilities = set()
    
    # Symulowana pozycja (aktualizowana po każdym MOVE)
    current_pos = unit["position"].copy()
    
    for action_num in range(max_actions):
        action = decide_single_action(
            unit, 
            all_units, 
            game_map, 
            current_pos, 
            used_abilities
        )
        
        if action:
            actions.append(action)
            
            # Aktualizuj stan
            if action["type"] == "MOVE":
                # Przelicz nową pozycję
                direction = action["direction"]
                dx, dy = direction_to_delta(direction)
                current_pos = [current_pos[0] + dx, current_pos[1] + dy]
            elif action["type"] == "ABILITY":
                used_abilities.add(action["slot"])
            elif action["type"] in ["MELEE", "BRACE", "FOCUS"]:
                used_abilities.add(action["type"])
    
    return actions


def direction_to_delta(direction):
    """Konwertuje kierunek na (dx, dy)."""
    deltas = {
        "N": (0, 1),
        "NE": (1, 1),
        "E": (1, 0),
        "SE": (1, -1),
        "S": (0, -1),
        "SW": (-1, -1),
        "W": (-1, 0),
        "NW": (-1, 1)
    }
    return deltas.get(direction, (0, 0))


def decide_single_action(unit, all_units, game_map, current_pos, used_abilities):
    """
    Decyduje o pojedynczej akcji.
    Używa drzewka decyzyjnego z randomizacją.
    Jeśli wszystkie poziomy skipnięte losowo — retry do 100 razy.
    Zwraca: (action_dict, decision_log_list) lub (None, decision_log_list)
    """
    for retry in range(100):
        result, dlog, had_skip = _run_decision_tree(unit, all_units, game_map, current_pos, used_abilities)
        if result is not None:
            if retry > 0:
                dlog.insert(0, f"(retry #{retry})")
            return result, dlog
        if not had_skip:
            # Nie było skipów a i tak nic nie wybrano — naprawdę nic do zrobienia
            return None, dlog
    # 100 prób — poddaj się
    dlog.append("(100 retries, poddaję się)")
    return None, dlog


def _run_decision_tree(unit, all_units, game_map, current_pos, used_abilities):
    """
    Pojedynczy przelot przez drzewko decyzyjne.
    Zwraca: (action_or_None, dlog, had_skip)
    """
    dlog = []
    had_skip = False
    
    # === POZIOM 0: TAUNT (Priorytet absolutny) ===
    taunt_source = None
    for s in unit.get("statuses", []):
        if s["type"] == "TAUNTED":
            taunt_source = s.get("source")
            break
            
    if taunt_source:
        source_unit = next((u for u in all_units if u["name"] == taunt_source), None)
        if source_unit:
            dist = get_distance(current_pos, source_unit["position"])
            
            best_off = get_available_offensive_for_range(unit, dist, used_abilities, source_unit, game_map)
            if best_off:
                dlog.append(f"P0 TAUNT: atak na {source_unit['name']}")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": best_off["slot"],
                    "target": source_unit["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            
            if can_unit_move(unit):
                d = get_direction_towards(current_pos, source_unit["position"])
                if d:
                    dlog.append(f"P0 TAUNT: ruch do {source_unit['name']}")
                    return {"type": "MOVE", "direction": d}, dlog, had_skip
            
            dlog.append("P0 TAUNT: brak możliwości")
            return None, dlog, had_skip
        
    # === POZIOM 1: Krytyczny stan (CON <= 2) ===
    if unit["con"] <= 2:
        if should_skip():
            dlog.append("P1 krytyczny: SKIP (losowy)")
            had_skip = True
        else:
            healing = get_healing_abilities(unit)
            healing = [h for h in healing if h["slot"] not in used_abilities]
            # Filtruj duplikaty (np. żeby nie nałożyć na siebie drugiego TEMP_HP)
            healing = [h for h in healing if not target_has_effect(unit, h["ability"].get("name", ""))]
            
            if healing:
                dlog.append(f"P1 krytyczny: self-heal [{healing[0]['slot']}]")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": healing[0]["slot"],
                    "target": unit["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P1 krytyczny: brak heali")
    else:
        dlog.append("P1 krytyczny: HP OK")
    
    # === POZIOM 2: Wróg w zasięgu 0 (melee) ===
    enemies_range_0 = get_enemies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
    
    if enemies_range_0:
        if should_skip():
            dlog.append(f"P2 wróg r0: SKIP (losowy, {len(enemies_range_0)} wrogów)")
            had_skip = True
        else:
            target = select_best_target(enemies_range_0, unit)
            ability = get_available_offensive_for_range(unit, 0, used_abilities, target, game_map)
            
            if ability:
                dlog.append(f"P2 wróg r0: atak [{ability['slot']}] na {target['name']}")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": ability["slot"],
                    "target": target["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append(f"P2 wróg r0: brak zdolności (0% lub CD)")
    else:
        dlog.append("P2 wróg r0: brak wrogów")
    
    # === POZIOM 3: Wróg w zasięgu 1 (ranged) ===
    enemies_range_1 = get_enemies_at_range(unit, all_units, current_pos, 1)
    
    if enemies_range_1:
        if should_skip():
            dlog.append(f"P3 wróg r1: SKIP (losowy, {len(enemies_range_1)} wrogów)")
            had_skip = True
        else:
            target = select_best_target(enemies_range_1, unit)
            ability = get_available_offensive_for_range(unit, 1, used_abilities, target, game_map)
            
            if ability:
                dlog.append(f"P3 wróg r1: atak [{ability['slot']}] na {target['name']}")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": ability["slot"],
                    "target": target["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append(f"P3 wróg r1: brak zdolności (0% lub CD)")
    else:
        dlog.append("P3 wróg r1: brak wrogów")
    
    # === POZIOM 4: Sojusznik w potrzebie (CON <= 2) w zasięgu ===
    allies = get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
    allies_hurt = [a for a in allies if a["con"] <= 2]
    
    if allies_hurt:
        if should_skip():
            dlog.append(f"P4 ally heal: SKIP (losowy, {len(allies_hurt)} rannych)")
            had_skip = True
        else:
            healing = get_healing_abilities(unit)
            healing = [h for h in healing if h["slot"] not in used_abilities]
            healing = [h for h in healing if ability_can_reach(h["ability"], 0)]
            
            if healing:
                for h in healing:
                    ab_name = h["ability"].get("name", "")
                    valid_targets = [a for a in allies_hurt if not target_has_effect(a, ab_name)]
                    if valid_targets:
                        target = min(valid_targets, key=lambda a: a["con"])
                        dlog.append(f"P4 ally heal: [{h['slot']}] na {target['name']}")
                        return {
                            "type": "ABILITY",
                            "slot": h["slot"],
                            "target": target["name"]
                        }, dlog, had_skip
                dlog.append("P4 ally heal: wszyscy ranni ally mają już te efekty")
            else:
                dlog.append("P4 ally heal: brak heali")
    else:
        dlog.append("P4 ally heal: brak rannych ally")

    # === POZIOM 4.5: CLEANSE na siebie lub ally ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P4.5 CLEANSE: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        cleanse_abs = get_cleanse_abilities(unit)
        cleanse_abs = [c for c in cleanse_abs if c["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        if cleanse_abs:
            # Priorytet: self jeśli mam negatywne, potem ally na polu
            cleanse_target = None
            if has_negative_effects(unit):
                cleanse_target = unit
            else:
                allies_here = get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
                debuffed_allies = [a for a in allies_here if has_negative_effects(a)]
                if debuffed_allies:
                    cleanse_target = max(debuffed_allies, key=lambda a: len(a.get("statuses", [])))
            
            if cleanse_target:
                dlog.append(f"P4.5 CLEANSE: [{cleanse_abs[0]['slot']}] na {cleanse_target['name']}")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": cleanse_abs[0]["slot"],
                    "target": cleanse_target["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P4.5 CLEANSE: brak celów z debuffami")
        else:
            dlog.append("P4.5 CLEANSE: brak zdolności")

    # === POZIOM 4.6: DISPEL na wrogu ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P4.6 DISPEL: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        dispel_abs = get_dispel_abilities(unit)
        dispel_abs = [d for d in dispel_abs if d["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        if dispel_abs:
            # Szukaj wrogów z pozytywnymi efektami w zasięgu 0-1
            dispel_target = None
            enemies_here = get_enemies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
            buffed_enemies = [e for e in enemies_here if has_positive_effects(e)]
            if buffed_enemies:
                dispel_target = buffed_enemies[0]
            else:
                enemies_near = get_enemies_at_range(unit, all_units, current_pos, 1)
                buffed_near = [e for e in enemies_near if has_positive_effects(e)]
                if buffed_near:
                    # Sprawdź czy zdolność ma zasięg 1
                    for da in dispel_abs:
                        if ability_can_reach(da["ability"], 1):
                            dispel_target = buffed_near[0]
                            break
            
            if dispel_target:
                dlog.append(f"P4.6 DISPEL: [{dispel_abs[0]['slot']}] na {dispel_target['name']}")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": dispel_abs[0]["slot"],
                    "target": dispel_target["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P4.6 DISPEL: brak wrogów z buffami")
        else:
            dlog.append("P4.6 DISPEL: brak zdolności")
            

    # === POZIOM 5.0: RES_TEMP buff na ally ===
    all_enemies = get_all_known_enemies(unit, all_units)
    
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P5.0 RES_TEMP ally: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        buffs = get_res_temp_abilities(unit)
        buffs = [b for b in buffs if b["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        if buffs:
            allies_here = get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
            if allies_here:
                for b in buffs:
                    ab_name = b["ability"].get("name", "")
                    valid = [a for a in allies_here if not target_has_effect(a, ab_name)]
                    if valid:
                        tgt = min(valid, key=lambda a: a["con"])
                        dlog.append(f"P5.0 RES_TEMP ally: [{b['slot']}] na {tgt['name']}")
                        return {
                            "type": "ABILITY",
                            "slot": b["slot"],
                            "target": tgt["name"]
                        }, dlog, had_skip
                dlog.append("P5.0 RES_TEMP ally: wszyscy ally już mają te efekty")
            else:
                dlog.append("P5.0 RES_TEMP ally: brak ally na polu")
        else:
            dlog.append("P5.0 RES_TEMP ally: brak zdolności")

    # === POZIOM 5.1: RES buff (SHIELD/PIERCE) na ally ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P5.1 RES buff ally: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        buffs = get_res_buff_abilities(unit)
        buffs = [b for b in buffs if b["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        if buffs:
            buffs.sort(key=lambda b: 0 if "SHIELD" in b["ability"].get("type", "") else 1)
            allies_here = get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
            if allies_here:
                for b in buffs:
                    ab_name = b["ability"].get("name", "")
                    valid = [a for a in allies_here if not target_has_effect(a, ab_name)]
                    if valid:
                        tgt = min(valid, key=lambda a: a["con"])
                        dlog.append(f"P5.1 RES buff ally: [{b['slot']}] na {tgt['name']}")
                        return {
                            "type": "ABILITY",
                            "slot": b["slot"],
                            "target": tgt["name"]
                        }, dlog, had_skip
                dlog.append("P5.1 RES buff ally: wszyscy ally już mają te efekty")
            else:
                dlog.append("P5.1 RES buff ally: brak ally na polu")
        else:
            dlog.append("P5.1 RES buff ally: brak zdolności")

    
    # === POZIOM 5.3: SETUP (budowanie stacków do EMPOWERED) ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P5.3 SETUP: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        setup_abilities = [item for item in get_available_abilities(unit) 
                          if item["ability"].get("type") == "SETUP" 
                          and item["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        if setup_abilities and unit.get("setup_stacks", 0) < 3:
            has_empowered = any(
                unit["abilities"][s].get("empowered") for s in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]
            )
            if has_empowered:
                dlog.append(f"P5.3 SETUP: [{setup_abilities[0]['slot']}] ({unit.get('setup_stacks',0)}/3)")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": setup_abilities[0]["slot"],
                    "target": unit["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P5.3 SETUP: brak EMPOWERED zdolności")
        else:
            dlog.append("P5.3 SETUP: brak/max stacks")

    # === POZIOM 5.5: RES_TEMP / SHIELD fallback na siebie ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P5.5 self-buff: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        buffs = get_res_temp_abilities(unit)
        buffs = [b for b in buffs if b["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        buffs = [b for b in buffs if not target_has_effect(unit, b["ability"].get("name", ""))]
        if buffs:
            dlog.append(f"P5.5 self-buff: RES_TEMP [{buffs[0]['slot']}]")
            return {
                "type": "ABILITY",
                "slot": buffs[0]["slot"],
                "target": unit["name"]
            }, dlog, had_skip
        
        buffs = get_res_buff_abilities(unit)
        buffs = [b for b in buffs if b["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        buffs = [b for b in buffs if not target_has_effect(unit, b["ability"].get("name", ""))]
        if buffs:
            buffs.sort(key=lambda b: 0 if "SHIELD" in b["ability"].get("type", "") else 1)
            dlog.append(f"P5.5 self-buff: RES [{buffs[0]['slot']}]")
            return {
                "type": "ABILITY",
                "slot": buffs[0]["slot"],
                "target": unit["name"]
            }, dlog, had_skip
        
        dlog.append("P5.5 self-buff: brak zdolności")

    # === POZIOM 5.7: REFLECT (lustrzana tarcza na siebie lub ally) ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P5.7 REFLECT: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        reflect_abs = get_reflect_abilities(unit)
        reflect_abs = [r for r in reflect_abs if r["slot"] not in used_abilities]
        if reflect_abs:
            reflect_target = None
            for ra in reflect_abs:
                ab_range = ra["ability"].get("range", 0)
                
                # range 0: tylko self
                # range "0-1": self lub ally
                # range 1: tylko ally
                can_self = ab_range == 0 or ab_range == "0-1"
                can_ally = ab_range == 1 or ab_range == "0-1"
                
                # Priorytet: self jeśli dozwolone i brak tej konkretnej tarczy
                if can_self and not target_has_effect(unit, ra["ability"].get("name", "")):
                    reflect_target = unit
                    reflect_ability = ra
                    break
                
                # Ally na polu bez tej konkretnej tarczy
                if can_ally:
                    allies_here = get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
                    allies_no_reflect = [a for a in allies_here if not target_has_effect(a, ra["ability"].get("name", ""))]
                    if allies_no_reflect:
                        reflect_target = min(allies_no_reflect, key=lambda a: a["con"])
                        reflect_ability = ra
                        break
            
            if reflect_target:
                dlog.append(f"P5.7 REFLECT: [{reflect_ability['slot']}] na {reflect_target['name']}")
                return {
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": reflect_ability["slot"],
                    "target": reflect_target["name"]
                }, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P5.7 REFLECT: brak odpowiednich celów")
        else:
            dlog.append("P5.7 REFLECT: brak zdolności")

    # === POZIOM 6.5: Akcje uniwersalne (MELEE/BRACE/FOCUS) ===
    if should_skip():
        dlog.append("P6.5 universal: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        universal = decide_universal_action(unit, all_units, current_pos, used_abilities)
        if universal:
            dlog.append(f"P6.5 universal: {universal['type']}")
            return universal, dlog, had_skip
        else:
            dlog.append("P6.5 universal: brak dostępnych")

    # === POZIOM 5.8: Ruch do wroga (najbliższy, nie ruszaj się jeśli wróg na polu) ===
    if not can_unit_move(unit):
        dlog.append("P5.8 ruch do wroga: nie może się ruszać")
    elif not all_enemies:
        dlog.append("P5.8 ruch do wroga: brak wrogów")
    elif should_skip():
        dlog.append("P5.8 ruch do wroga: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        enemies_here = get_enemies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
        if enemies_here:
            dlog.append("P5.8 ruch do wroga: wróg już na polu, zostań")
            return None, dlog, had_skip
        
        enemy_by_dist = sorted(all_enemies, key=lambda e: get_distance(current_pos, e["position"]))
        for target_enemy in enemy_by_dist:
            if is_crowded(target_enemy["position"], all_units):
                dlog.append(f"P5.8 ruch do wroga: {target_enemy['name']} na zatłoczonym polu, pomijam")
                continue
            d = get_direction_towards(current_pos, target_enemy["position"])
            if d:
                dlog.append(f"P5.8 ruch do wroga: {d} → {target_enemy['name']} (dist {get_distance(current_pos, target_enemy['position'])})")
                return {"type": "MOVE", "direction": d}, dlog, had_skip
        dlog.append("P5.8 ruch do wroga: brak dostępnych (wszystkie zatłoczone lub brak kierunku)")

    # === POZIOM 6: Ruch do neutrala (najbliższy, nie ruszaj się jeśli neutral na polu) ===
    if not can_unit_move(unit):
        dlog.append("P6 ruch do neutrala: nie może się ruszać")
    elif should_skip():
        dlog.append("P6 ruch do neutrala: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        neutrals_here = get_neutrals_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
        if neutrals_here:
            dlog.append("P6 ruch do neutrala: neutral już na polu, zostań")
            return None, dlog, had_skip
        
        all_neutrals = [u for u in all_units if u["name"] != unit["name"] and get_effective_relationship(unit, u) == "NEUTRAL"]
        distant_neutrals = [n for n in all_neutrals if get_distance(current_pos, n["position"]) > 0]
        # Odfiltruj zatłoczone pola
        distant_neutrals = [n for n in distant_neutrals if not is_crowded(n["position"], all_units)]
        
        if distant_neutrals:
            closest_neutral = min(distant_neutrals, key=lambda n: get_distance(current_pos, n["position"]))
            d = get_direction_towards(current_pos, closest_neutral["position"])
            if d:
                dlog.append(f"P6 ruch do neutrala: {d} → {closest_neutral['name']}")
                return {"type": "MOVE", "direction": d}, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P6 ruch do neutrala: brak kierunku")
        else:
            dlog.append("P6 ruch do neutrala: brak neutralnych (lub wszystkie zatłoczone)")

    # === POZIOM 6.2: Ruch do ally (najbliższy, nie ruszaj się jeśli ally na polu) ===
    if not can_unit_move(unit):
        dlog.append("P6.2 ruch do ally: nie może się ruszać")
    elif should_skip():
        dlog.append("P6.2 ruch do ally: SKIP (losowy)")
        had_skip = True
    else:
        allies_here = get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
        if allies_here:
            dlog.append("P6.2 ruch do ally: ally już na polu, zostań")
            return None, dlog, had_skip
        
        all_allies = [u for u in all_units if u["name"] != unit["name"] and get_effective_relationship(unit, u) == "ALLY"]
        distant_allies = [a for a in all_allies if get_distance(current_pos, a["position"]) > 0]
        # Odfiltruj zatłoczone pola
        distant_allies = [a for a in distant_allies if not is_crowded(a["position"], all_units)]
        
        if distant_allies:
            target_ally = min(distant_allies, key=lambda a: float(a["con"]))
            d = get_direction_towards(current_pos, target_ally["position"])
            if d:
                dlog.append(f"P6.2 ruch do ally: {d} → {target_ally['name']} ({round(float(target_ally['con']), 2)} HP)")
                return {"type": "MOVE", "direction": d}, dlog, had_skip
            else:
                dlog.append("P6.2 ruch do ally: brak kierunku")
        else:
            dlog.append("P6.2 ruch do ally: brak odległych ally (lub wszystkie zatłoczone)")

    # === POZIOM 7: Systematyczna eksploracja ===
    if can_unit_move(unit):
        if should_skip():
            dlog.append("P7 eksploracja: SKIP (losowy)")
            had_skip = True
        else:
            target_pos = get_nearest_undiscovered(unit, game_map)
            
            if target_pos:
                d = get_direction_towards(current_pos, target_pos)
                if d:
                    dlog.append(f"P7 eksploracja: {d} → [{target_pos[0]},{target_pos[1]}]")
                    return {"type": "MOVE", "direction": d}, dlog, had_skip
            
            direction = get_random_direction()
            dlog.append(f"P7 losowy ruch: {direction} (mapa odkryta)")
            return {
                "type": "MOVE",
                "direction": direction
            }, dlog, had_skip
    
    dlog.append("P7: nie może się ruszać, brak akcji")
    return None, dlog, had_skip


# === FUNKCJE POMOCNICZE ===
def is_crowded(pos, all_units, threshold=3):
    """Sprawdza czy na danym polu jest >= threshold jednostek (żywych)."""
    count = 0
    for u in all_units:
        if u["con"] > 0 and u["position"] == list(pos):
            count += 1
    return count >= threshold


def select_best_target(enemies, attacker):
    """
    Wybiera najlepszy cel z listy wrogów.
    Priorytet: najniższe CON (dobijanie), potem losowo.
    """
    if not enemies:
        return None
    
    # Sortuj po CON (najniższe pierwsze)
    sorted_enemies = sorted(enemies, key=lambda e: float(e["con"]))
    
    # Jeśli kilku ma takie samo najniższe CON, losuj spośród nich
    lowest_con = float(sorted_enemies[0]["con"])
    lowest_group = [e for e in sorted_enemies if float(e["con"]) == lowest_con]
    
    return random.choice(lowest_group)


def get_enemies_at_position(unit, all_units, pos):
    """Zwraca wrogów na dokładnie tej pozycji (żywych)."""
    enemies = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        if float(other.get("con", 0)) <= 0:
            continue
        if other["position"] == pos or other["position"] == list(pos):
            rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
            if rel == "ENEMY":
                enemies.append(other)
    return enemies


def get_enemies_at_range(unit, all_units, pos, range_val):
    """Zwraca wrogów w dokładnie danym zasięgu (żywych)."""
    enemies = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        if float(other.get("con", 0)) <= 0:
            continue
        dist = get_distance(pos, other["position"])
        if dist == range_val:
            rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
            if rel == "ENEMY":
                enemies.append(other)
    return enemies


def get_allies_at_position(unit, all_units, pos):
    """Zwraca sojuszników na dokładnie tej pozycji (żywych)."""
    allies = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        if float(other.get("con", 0)) <= 0:
            continue
        if other["position"] == pos or other["position"] == list(pos):
            rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
            if rel == "ALLY":
                allies.append(other)
    return allies


def get_neutrals_at_position(unit, all_units, pos):
    """Zwraca neutralnych na dokładnie tej pozycji (żywych)."""
    neutrals = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        if float(other.get("con", 0)) <= 0:
            continue
        if other["position"] == pos or other["position"] == list(pos):
            rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
            if rel == "NEUTRAL":
                neutrals.append(other)
    return neutrals


def get_neutrals_at_range(unit, all_units, pos, range_val):
    """Zwraca neutralnych w dokładnie danym zasięgu."""
    neutrals = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        dist = get_distance(pos, other["position"])
        if dist == range_val:
            rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
            if rel == "NEUTRAL":
                neutrals.append(other)
    return neutrals


def get_all_known_enemies(unit, all_units):
    """Zwraca wszystkich znanych wrogów (żywych)."""
    enemies = []
    for other in all_units:
        if other["name"] == unit["name"]:
            continue
        if float(other.get("con", 0)) <= 0:
            continue
        rel = get_effective_relationship(unit, other)
        if rel == "ENEMY":
            enemies.append(other)
    return enemies


def get_closest_enemy(unit, all_units, current_pos):
    """Zwraca najbliższego wroga."""
    enemies = get_all_known_enemies(unit, all_units)
    
    if not enemies:
        return None
    
    closest = min(enemies, key=lambda e: get_distance(current_pos, e["position"]))
    return closest


def get_nearest_undiscovered(unit, game_map):
    """Zwraca najbliższe nieodkryte pole (None w game_map) lub None."""
    ux, uy = unit["position"]
    undiscovered = []
    
    # Znajdź wszystkie nieodkryte
    for y in range(len(game_map)):
        for x in range(len(game_map[0])):
            if game_map[y][x] is None:
                dist = max(abs(x - ux), abs(y - uy))
                undiscovered.append(((x, y), dist))
    
    if not undiscovered:
        return None
    
    # Sortuj po odległości
    undiscovered.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
    return undiscovered[0][0]  # (x, y)


def get_available_offensive_for_range(unit, range_val, used_abilities, target=None, game_map=None):
    """
    Zwraca najlepszą ofensywną zdolność dla danego zasięgu (DD/DOT/CC/RES debuff),
    wybierając po wartości oczekiwanej (score). Pomija hit_chance==0.
    """
    offensive = get_offensive_abilities(unit)

    # Pobierz teren
    terrain_id = None
    if game_map:
        x, y = unit["position"]
        if 0 <= y < len(game_map) and 0 <= x < len(game_map[0]):
            terrain_id = game_map[y][x]

    if not target:
        return None

    def _range_ok(ability_range, ab=None):
        if ability_range == "0-1":
            return True
        # AOE: range = lokacja docelowa, effect_range = zasięg efektu od lokacji
        # Zdolność jest użyteczna jeśli wróg w range + effect_range od castera
        if ab and ab.get("type") == "AOE":
            eff_range = int(ab.get("effect_range", 0))
            base_range = int(ability_range) if isinstance(ability_range, (int, float)) else 0
            return range_val <= base_range + eff_range
        return ability_range == range_val

    def _cc_weight(t):
        # STUN > SILENCE/DISARM > ROOT (heurystyka)
        if t == "STUN":
            return 1.5
        if t in ["SILENCE", "DISARM"]:
            return 1.2
        if t == "ROOT":
            return 1.0
        return 1.0

    candidates = []
    for item in offensive:
        slot = item["slot"]
        if slot in used_abilities:
            continue

        ab = item["ability"]
        ability_type = ab.get("type", "")
        ability_range = ab.get("range", 0)

        if not _range_ok(ability_range, ab):
            continue

        # DISPEL: pomijaj jeśli cel nie ma pozytywnych efektów
        if ability_type == "DISPEL" and not has_positive_effects(target):
            continue
        if ability_type == "AOE" and ab.get("aoe_type") == "DISPEL" and not has_positive_effects(target):
            continue
        
        # Duplikat: pomijaj jeśli cel już ma efekt tej zdolności
        ab_name = ab.get("name", "")
        if ab_name and target_has_effect(target, ab_name):
            continue

        hit_chance = calculate_hit_chance(unit, target, slot, terrain_id)
        if hit_chance <= 0:
            continue

        score = 0.0

        if ability_type == "CON_DMG":
            dmg = float(ab.get("damage", ab.get("value", 1.0)))
            score = hit_chance * dmg

        elif ability_type == "DOT":
            total = float(ab.get("total_damage", ab.get("total", 1.5)))
            score = hit_chance * total

        elif ability_type in ["ROOT", "STUN", "SILENCE", "DISARM"]:
            bac = float(ab.get("bac", 50))
            if slot in ["B", "D"]:
                bac *= 0.5
            score = hit_chance * (bac / 100.0) * _cc_weight(ability_type) * 100.0

        elif ability_type in ["CHARM", "FEAR"]:
            duration = int(ab.get("duration", 1))
            bac = 100.0 / float(max(1, duration))
            if slot in ["B", "D"]:
                bac *= 0.5
            weight = 2.0 if ability_type == "CHARM" else 1.8
            score = hit_chance * (bac / 100.0) * weight * 100.0

        elif ability_type == "DRAINLIFE":
            dmg = float(ab.get("damage", ab.get("value", 0.5)))
            dmg = min(dmg, 0.5)
            score = hit_chance * dmg * 1.3  # Bonus za self-heal

        elif ability_type == "DRAINLIFE_DOT":
            total = float(ab.get("total_damage", ab.get("total", 0.75)))
            total = min(total, 0.75)
            score = hit_chance * total * 1.3  # Bonus za drain

        elif ability_type == "AOE":
            aoe_sub = ab.get("aoe_type", "DD")
            effect_range = int(ab.get("effect_range", 0))
            dmg_mod = 0.75 if effect_range == 0 else 0.50
            if ab.get("no_friendly_fire", False):
                dmg_mod *= 0.75

            if aoe_sub == "DRAINLIFE":
                dmg = min(float(ab.get("damage", ab.get("value", 0.5))), 0.5)
                score = hit_chance * dmg * dmg_mod * 1.5 * 1.3
            elif aoe_sub in ["CHARM", "FEAR"]:
                dur = int(ab.get("duration", 1))
                aoe_bac = (100.0 / float(max(1, dur))) * dmg_mod
                weight = 2.0 if aoe_sub == "CHARM" else 1.8
                score = hit_chance * (aoe_bac / 100.0) * weight * 100.0
            else:
                dmg = float(ab.get("damage", ab.get("value", 1.0)))
                score = hit_chance * dmg * dmg_mod * 1.5

        elif isinstance(ability_type, str) and ability_type.startswith("RES_") and (("VULN" in ability_type) or ("WEAKEN_ATTACK" in ability_type)):
            val = float(ab.get("value", 10.0))
            dur = float(ab.get("duration", 5))
            score = hit_chance * val * dur

        else:
            # fallback
            score = hit_chance

        candidates.append({"item": item, "hit_chance": hit_chance, "score": score})

    if not candidates:
        return None

    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
    return candidates[0]["item"]

def decide_universal_action(unit, all_units, current_pos, used_abilities):
    """
    Decyduje o akcji uniwersalnej (MELEE/BRACE).
    Wywoływane gdy normalne zdolności są niedostępne lub pominięte.
    Każda akcja max 1/aktywację.
    """
    has_stun = any(s["type"] == "STUN" for s in unit.get("statuses", []))
    if has_stun:
        return None
    
    used_melee = "MELEE" in used_abilities
    used_brace = "BRACE" in used_abilities
    
    # 1. MELEE: na wrogów w zasięgu 0 (wszystkich ENEMY)
    if not used_melee:
        enemies_here = get_enemies_at_position(unit, all_units, current_pos)
        
        if enemies_here:
            target = select_best_target(enemies_here, unit)
            if target:
                return {
                    "type": "MELEE",
                    "target": target["name"]
                }

    # 2. BRACE: zawsze jeśli nie użyto
    if not used_brace:
        return {
            "type": "BRACE"
        }
    
    return None
# ai_parser.py
# Pancerny parser odpowiedzi AI dla Monster Engine 5.0

import re
import random


# Synonimy kierunków relacji (lowercase)
# Delta za zmianę relacji AI: ±15 (obowiązkowa zmiana co aktywację)
AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA = 15

_WZROST_SYNONYMS = {
    "wzrost", "wzrost relacji", "poprawa", "up", "wyżej", "podnieś",
    "polepszenie", "lepiej", "plus", "pozytywny", "pozytywna",
    "improvement", "increase", "rise", "growth", "better",
    "wrostl", "wzrost!", "w górę", "rośnie", "poprawia się"
}

_SPADEK_SYNONYMS = {
    "spadek", "spadek relacji", "pogorszenie", "down", "obniż", "mniej",
    "gorzej", "minus", "negatywny", "negatywna", "niżej",
    "decrease", "drop", "fall", "decline", "worse", "lower",
    "spadek!", "w dół", "spada", "pogarsza się", "maleje"
}

_NEUTRAL_SYNONYMS = {
    "neutral", "neutralny", "neutralna", "bez zmian", "brak zmian",
    "zostaw", "nie zmieniaj", "none", "no change", "unchanged",
    "brak", "zero", "0", "bez zmiany", "nie zmienia się",
    "neutralnie", "neutralne", "stabline", "stabilnie", "stabilna"
}


def _classify_direction(raw_text):
    """
    Klasyfikuje kierunek zmiany relacji z surowego tekstu AI.
    Zwraca: +15, -15. None jeśli nie rozpoznano.
    NEUTRAL traktowany jako losowy WZROST/SPADEK (AI nie powinno go używać).
    
    Strategia: najpierw próbuj pierwsze słowo (przed - , ; :), potem cały tekst.
    """
    cleaned = raw_text.strip().lower()
    # Usuń interpunkcję końcową
    cleaned = cleaned.rstrip(".,!;:")
    
    # Wyodrębnij pierwsze słowo kluczowe (przed myślnikiem, przecinkiem, średnikiem itp.)
    first_word = re.split(r'[\s\-,;:]+', cleaned)[0].strip() if cleaned else ""
    
    # Priorytet 1: pierwsze słowo (najczęstszy format: "WZROST - opis...")
    if first_word in _WZROST_SYNONYMS:
        return +AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA
    if first_word in _SPADEK_SYNONYMS:
        return -AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA
    if first_word in _NEUTRAL_SYNONYMS:
        return random.choice([+AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA, -AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA])
    
    # Priorytet 2: exact match całego tekstu
    if cleaned in _WZROST_SYNONYMS:
        return +AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA
    if cleaned in _SPADEK_SYNONYMS:
        return -AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA
    if cleaned in _NEUTRAL_SYNONYMS:
        return random.choice([+AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA, -AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA])
    
    # Priorytet 3: fuzzy — synonim zawarty w tekście
    for syn in _WZROST_SYNONYMS:
        if syn in cleaned or cleaned in syn:
            return +AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA
    for syn in _SPADEK_SYNONYMS:
        if syn in cleaned or cleaned in syn:
            return -AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA
    for syn in _NEUTRAL_SYNONYMS:
        if syn in cleaned or cleaned in syn:
            return random.choice([+AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA, -AI_RELATIONSHIP_DELTA])
    
    return None


def _clean_name(raw_name):
    """Czyści imię z formatowania markdown i białych znaków."""
    name = raw_name.strip()
    # Usuń markdown bold/italic
    name = re.sub(r'\*+', '', name)
    # Usuń leading bullet points, dashes, numbers
    name = re.sub(r'^[-–—•·\d.)\]]+\s*', '', name)
    # Trim
    name = name.strip()
    return name


def _match_name_to_units(raw_name, unit_names):
    """
    Dopasowuje surowe imię z AI do listy znanych jednostek.
    Strategia: porównaj pierwsze 4 znaki (lowercase).
    
    Zwraca: dopasowana nazwa lub None.
    """
    cleaned = _clean_name(raw_name).lower()
    
    if not cleaned:
        return None
    
    # 1. Exact match (case insensitive)
    for name in unit_names:
        if name.lower() == cleaned:
            return name
    
    # 2. Pierwsze 4 znaki
    prefix = cleaned[:4]
    matches = [n for n in unit_names if n.lower().startswith(prefix)]
    if len(matches) == 1:
        return matches[0]
    
    # 3. Zawieranie (AI skróciło lub dodało tekst)
    matches = [n for n in unit_names if cleaned in n.lower() or n.lower() in cleaned]
    if len(matches) == 1:
        return matches[0]
    
    # 4. Pierwsze 3 znaki (bardziej agresywne)
    if len(prefix) >= 3:
        prefix3 = cleaned[:3]
        matches = [n for n in unit_names if n.lower().startswith(prefix3)]
        if len(matches) == 1:
            return matches[0]
    
    return None


def parse_relationship_changes(ai_response, known_unit_names=None):
    """
    Parsuje sekcję ### ZMIANA RELACJI ### z odpowiedzi AI.
    
    Pancerny parser:
    - Toleruje puste linie, formatowanie markdown, bullet points
    - Dopasowuje imiona po pierwszych 4 znakach
    - Rozpoznaje synonimy kierunków (WZROST/SPADEK/NEUTRAL)
    
    Args:
        ai_response: pełna odpowiedź AI (string)
        known_unit_names: lista znanych nazw jednostek (opcjonalna, do fuzzy match)
    
    Zwraca: dict {"Imię": delta_int, ...}
        WZROST = +20, SPADEK = -20, NEUTRAL = 0
    """
    results = {}
    
    if not ai_response:
        return results
    
    # Znajdź sekcję ZMIANA RELACJI (różne warianty nagłówka)
    section_patterns = [
        r'###\s*ZMIANA RELACJI\s*###',
        r'\*\*\s*ZMIANA RELACJI\s*\*\*',
        r'ZMIANA RELACJI\s*:',
        r'###\s*ZMIANA\s+RELACJI',
        r'RELACJE?\s*:?\s*$',
    ]
    
    match = None
    for pattern in section_patterns:
        match = re.search(pattern, ai_response, re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE)
        if match:
            break
    
    if not match:
        return results
    
    # Weź tekst po nagłówku sekcji
    section_text = ai_response[match.end():]
    
    # Separatory między imieniem a kierunkiem
    # Obsługuje: ->, →, :, –, —, =
    split_pattern = r'\s*(?:->|→|:|–|—|=)\s*'
    
    for line in section_text.split('\n'):
        line = line.strip()
        
        # Stop na kolejnym nagłówku markdown
        if line.startswith('###') and 'ZMIANA' not in line.upper():
            break
        
        # Pomiń puste linie i dekoracje
        if not line or line.startswith('```') or line == '---':
            continue
        
        # Pomiń jeśli linia to sam nagłówek
        if re.match(r'^\*?\*?\s*ZMIANA\s+RELACJI', line, re.IGNORECASE):
            continue
        
        # Spróbuj rozdzielić na imię i kierunek
        parts = re.split(split_pattern, line, maxsplit=1)
        
        if len(parts) != 2:
            continue
        
        raw_name = parts[0]
        raw_direction = parts[1]
        
        # Wyczyść imię
        name = _clean_name(raw_name)
        if not name or len(name) < 2:
            continue
        
        # Klasyfikuj kierunek
        delta = _classify_direction(raw_direction)
        if delta is None:
            continue
        
        # Dopasuj do znanych jednostek (jeśli podano listę)
        if known_unit_names:
            matched_name = _match_name_to_units(name, known_unit_names)
            if matched_name:
                results[matched_name] = delta
            # Jeśli nie dopasowano — pomijaj (nie zgadujemy)
        else:
            results[name] = delta
    
    return results

# Monster Engine 5.0 - Interfejs Streamlit (Python AI + Weryfikator)

import streamlit as st
import json
import random
import time
import os
from datetime import datetime

import state_manager as sm
import turn_manager as tm
import prompts

    st.session_state["pending_action"] = None

world = sm.get_world()

# === SIDEBAR ===
st.sidebar.title("⚔️ Monster Engine 5.0")

# --- Sterowanie Turą ---
if world:
    st.sidebar.subheader("⏳ Sterowanie")
    
    if st.sidebar.button("▶️ NASTĘPNA TURA", type="primary", use_container_width=True):
        if world["units"]:
            result = tm.run_turn_cycle()
            if result:
                dead_names = result.get("dead_units", [])
                
                if result["combat_occurred"]:
                    # Była walka lub śmierć - przygotuj prompt dla Weryfikatora
                    all_units = list(world["units"].values())
                    verifier_prompt = tm.build_verifier_prompt(
                        result["unit"],
                        result["action_results"],
                        all_units,
                        world["game_map"],
                        result.get("start_position"),
                        result.get("con_after_ticks"),
                        result.get("tick_events"),
                        result.get("start_visible")
                    )
                    st.session_state["pending_action"] = {
                        "type": "VERIFY_COMBAT",
                        "data": {
                            "unit": result["unit"],
                            "action_results": result["action_results"],
                            "verifier_prompt": verifier_prompt,
                            "dead_units": dead_names
                        }
                    }
                else:
                    # Brak walki
                    if dead_names:
                        # Są martwi mimo braku walki — przygotuj epitafium
                        dead_for_memory = []
                        for dn in dead_names:
                            du = sm.get_unit(dn)
                            if du:
                                dead_for_memory.append({
                                    "name": dn,
                                    "bio": du.get("bio", "Brak opisu.")
                                })
                        if dead_for_memory:
                            st.session_state["pending_action"] = {
                                "type": "MEMORY_SESSION",
                                "data": {
                                    "dead_units": dead_for_memory,
                                    "current_index": 0
                                }
                            }
                        else:
                            st.session_state["pending_action"] = None
                    else:
                        st.session_state["pending_action"] = None
            st.rerun()
        else:
            st.sidebar.warning("Brak jednostek na arenie!")
    

st.sidebar.divider()

# --- Dodawanie Jednostek ---
if world:
    st.sidebar.subheader("👹 Dodaj Jednostkę")
    
    with st.sidebar.expander("📋 Wklej JSON jednostki"):
        # Klucz formularza pozwala wyczyścić po dodaniu
        if "json_input_key" not in st.session_state:
            st.session_state["json_input_key"] = 0

            "JSON jednostki:",
            height=200,
            placeholder='{"name": "...", "bio": "...", ...}',
            key=f"json_input_{st.session_state['json_input_key']}"
        )
        
        if st.button("➕ DODAJ JEDNOSTKĘ", use_container_width=True):
            if json_input:

                    unit_data = json.loads(json_input)

                    if missing:
                        st.error(f"Brakuje pól: {', '.join(missing)}")
                    else:
                        full_unit = create_unit(
                            name=unit_data["name"],

                                ability_b_name=added["abilities"]["B"].get("name", "?"),
                                ability_b_lore=added["abilities"]["B"].get("lore", "Brak opisu."),
                                ability_c_name=added["abilities"]["C"].get("name", "?"),
                                ability_c_lore=added["abilities"]["C"].get("lore", "Brak opisu."),
                                ability_d_name=added["abilities"]["D"].get("name", "?"),
                                ability_d_lore=added["abilities"]["D"].get("lore", "Brak opisu.")
                            )
                            st.session_state["pending_action"] = {
                                "type": "REGISTER_UNIT",
                                "data": {
                                    "unit_name": added["name"],
                                    "prompt": reg_prompt
                                }
                            }
                            
                            # Wyczyść pole JSON przez zmianę klucza

                            time.sleep(0.5)
                            st.rerun()
                        else:

                    }
                    st.rerun()
                else:
                    sm.remove_unit(unit_to_remove)
                    st.rerun()

            st.write("**Zmień domenę:**")
            unit_to_edit = st.selectbox("Jednostka:", list(world["units"].keys()), key="domain_edit_unit")

            current_domain = edit_unit["domains"][slot_to_edit]
