                    "duration": ability.get("duration", None)
                })

                aoe_target["temp_hp"] = float(aoe_target.get("temp_hp", 0)) + final_value
                aoe_target["con"] = float(aoe_target["con"]) + final_value
                tr["target_con_after"] = float(aoe_target["con"])
                any_success = True

                hit_results.append(tr)

        # ======== AOE CLEANSE ========
        elif aoe_type == "CLEANSE":
            bac = 75.0
            if ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice:
                bac = min(bac * 2.0, 100.0)
            bac *= dmg_modifier
            if slot in ["B", "D"]:
                bac *= 0.5
            result["details"]["bac"] = bac

            for aoe_target in aoe_targets:
                tr = {
                    "name": aoe_target["name"],
                    "bio": aoe_target.get("bio", "Brak opisu."),
                    "domains": aoe_target["domains"].copy(),
                    "target_con_after": float(aoe_target["con"]),
                    "target_max_con": float(aoe_target["max_con"]),
                    "effect": "CLEANSE",
                    "removed": [],
                    "bac_roll": None,
                    "bac_success": None
                }

                roll = random.randint(1, 100)
                tr["bac_roll"] = roll

                if roll <= bac:
                    tr["bac_success"] = True
                    removed = []
                    neg_statuses = ["STUN", "ROOT", "SILENCE", "DISARM", "CHARMED", "FEARED", "TAUNTED"]

                    kept_s = []
                    for s in aoe_target.get("statuses", []):
                        if s["type"] in neg_statuses:
                            removed.append(f"status:{s['type']}")
                        else:
                            kept_s.append(s)
                    aoe_target["statuses"] = kept_s

                    kept_r = []
                    for m in aoe_target.get("res_modifiers", []):
                        if m.get("mode") in ["VULN", "WEAKEN_ATTACK"]:
                            removed.append(f"res:{m.get('mode')}")
                        else:
                            kept_r.append(m)
                    aoe_target["res_modifiers"] = kept_r

                    kept_o = []
                    for e in aoe_target.get("ot_effects", []):
                        if e.get("type") in ["DOT", "DRAIN_DOT"]:
                            removed.append(f"ot:{e.get('type')}")
                        else:
                            kept_o.append(e)
                    aoe_target["ot_effects"] = kept_o

                    tr["removed"] = removed
                    tr["removed_count"] = len(removed)
                    if removed:
                        any_success = True
                else:
                    tr["bac_success"] = False
                    tr["removed_count"] = 0

                hit_results.append(tr)

        # ======== AOE DISPEL ========
        elif aoe_type == "DISPEL":
            bac = 75.0
            if ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice:
                bac = min(bac * 2.0, 100.0)
            bac *= dmg_modifier
            if slot in ["B", "D"]:
                bac *= 0.5
            result["details"]["bac"] = bac

            for aoe_target in aoe_targets:
                tr = {
                    "name": aoe_target["name"],
                    "bio": aoe_target.get("bio", "Brak opisu."),
                    "domains": aoe_target["domains"].copy(),
                    "target_con_after": float(aoe_target["con"]),
                    "target_max_con": float(aoe_target["max_con"]),
                    "effect": "DISPEL",
                    "removed": [],
                    "bac_roll": None,
                    "bac_success": None
                }

                roll = random.randint(1, 100)
                tr["bac_roll"] = roll

                if roll <= bac:
                    tr["bac_success"] = True
                    removed = []

                    kept_r = []
                    for m in aoe_target.get("res_modifiers", []):
                        if m.get("mode") in ["SHIELD", "PIERCE"] or m.get("type") == "RES_TEMP":
                            removed.append(f"res:{m.get('mode', m.get('type','?'))}")
                        else:
                            kept_r.append(m)
                    aoe_target["res_modifiers"] = kept_r

                    kept_o = []
                    for e in aoe_target.get("ot_effects", []):
                        if e.get("type") == "HOT":
                            removed.append(f"ot:HOT")
                        else:
                            kept_o.append(e)
                    aoe_target["ot_effects"] = kept_o

                    if aoe_target.get("pending_heals"):
                        for p in aoe_target["pending_heals"]:
                            removed.append(f"pending_heal")
                        aoe_target["pending_heals"] = []

                    temp_hp = float(aoe_target.get("temp_hp", 0))
                    if temp_hp > 0:
                        aoe_target["con"] = float(aoe_target["con"]) - temp_hp
                        aoe_target["temp_hp"] = 0
                        removed.append(f"temp_hp:{temp_hp}")
                        tr["target_con_after"] = float(aoe_target["con"])

                    # Wyczyść trackowane efekty TEMP_HP
                    for thp in aoe_target.get("temp_hp_effects", []):
                        removed.append(f"temp_hp_effect:{thp.get('ability_name', '?')}")
                    aoe_target["temp_hp_effects"] = []

                    # Usuń REFLECT shields
                    for rs in aoe_target.get("reflect_shields", []):
                        removed.append(f"reflect:{rs.get('ability_name', '?')}")
                    aoe_target["reflect_shields"] = []

                    tr["removed"] = removed
                    tr["removed_count"] = len(removed)
                    if removed:
                        any_success = True
                else:
                    tr["bac_success"] = False
                    tr["removed_count"] = 0

                hit_results.append(tr)


        result["success"] = any_success
        result["details"]["hit_results"] = hit_results

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === DRAINLIFE (DD + self-heal) ===
    elif ability_type == "DRAINLIFE":
        if not target:
            result["error"] = "DRAINLIFE wymaga celu!"
            return result

        rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
        if rel_now == "ALLY":
            result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
            return result

        can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
        if not can:
            result["error"] = reason
            return result

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        roll = random.randint(1, 100)
        attack_result = resolve_attack(attacker, target, slot, roll, terrain_id)

        result["details"]["roll"] = roll
        result["details"]["hit_chance"] = attack_result["hit_chance"]
        result["details"]["breakdown"] = attack_result["breakdown"]
        result["details"]["is_critical"] = attack_result["is_critical"]

        if attack_result["success"]:
            damage = float(ability.get("damage", ability.get("value", 0.5)))
            cap = 1.0 if (ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice) else 0.5
            damage = min(damage, cap)

            # REFLECT check
            reflected, reflect_log = try_reflect(target, attacker, ability_type, result["details"], ability)
            result["details"]["reflect_log"] = reflect_log

            if reflected:
                # Odbicie: obrażenia + drain na atakującego, cel leczy się
                attacker_con_before = float(attacker["con"])
                _absorb_damage_through_temp(attacker, damage)

                target_temp = float(target.get("temp_hp", 0))
                target_real = float(target["con"]) - target_temp
                target_max = float(target["max_con"])
                actual_heal = min(damage, target_max - target_real)
                actual_heal = max(0.0, actual_heal)
                target["con"] = float(target["con"]) + actual_heal

                result["success"] = True
                result["details"]["reflected"] = True
                result["details"]["damage"] = damage
                result["details"]["heal_amount"] = actual_heal
                result["details"]["target_con_before"] = float(target["con"]) - actual_heal
                result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
                result["details"]["attacker_con_after"] = float(attacker["con"])

                if float(attacker["con"]) <= 0.0:
                    result["details"]["attacker_eliminated"] = True
            else:
                target_con_before = float(target["con"])
                _absorb_damage_through_temp(target, damage)

                attacker_temp = float(attacker.get("temp_hp", 0))
                attacker_real = float(attacker["con"]) - attacker_temp
                attacker_max = float(attacker["max_con"])
                actual_heal = min(damage, attacker_max - attacker_real)
                actual_heal = max(0.0, actual_heal)
                attacker["con"] = float(attacker["con"]) + actual_heal

                result["success"] = True
                result["details"]["damage"] = damage
                result["details"]["heal_amount"] = actual_heal
                result["details"]["target_con_before"] = target_con_before
                result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
                result["details"]["attacker_con_after"] = float(attacker["con"])

                if float(target["con"]) <= 0.0:
                    result["details"]["target_eliminated"] = True
            
        else:
            result["details"]["damage"] = 0.0
            result["details"]["heal_amount"] = 0.0

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === DRAINLIFE DOT ===
    elif ability_type == "DRAINLIFE_DOT":
        if not target:
            result["error"] = "DRAINLIFE_DOT wymaga celu!"
            return result

        rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
        if rel_now == "ALLY":
            result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
            return result

        can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
        if not can:
            result["error"] = reason
            return result

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        roll = random.randint(1, 100)
        dva_result = resolve_attack(attacker, target, slot, roll, terrain_id)

        result["details"]["roll"] = roll
        result["details"]["hit_chance"] = dva_result["hit_chance"]
        result["details"]["breakdown"] = dva_result["breakdown"]
        result["details"]["is_critical"] = dva_result["is_critical"]

        if not dva_result["success"]:
            result["success"] = False
            
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result

        # Blokuj duplikat DRAIN_DOT po ability_name
        already_has_drain = any(
            e.get("ability_name") == result["ability_name"]
            for e in target.get("ot_effects", [])
        )
        if already_has_drain:
            result["success"] = False
            result["error"] = f"{target['name']} już ma aktywny efekt {result['ability_name']}"
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result

        total = float(ability.get("total_damage", ability.get("total", 0.75)))
        cap = 1.5 if (ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice) else 0.75
        total = min(total, cap)
        duration = int(ability.get("duration", 5))
        tick = total / float(duration)

        # REFLECT check
        reflected, reflect_log = try_reflect(target, attacker, ability_type, result["details"], ability)
        result["details"]["reflect_log"] = reflect_log

        dot_victim = attacker if reflected else target

        if "ot_effects" not in dot_victim:
            dot_victim["ot_effects"] = []

        dot_victim["ot_effects"].append({
            "ability_name": result["ability_name"],
            "type": "DRAIN_DOT",
            "tick": tick,
            "duration": duration,
            "source": attacker["name"] if not reflected else target["name"]
        })

        result["success"] = True
        result["details"]["reflected"] = reflected
        result["details"]["total"] = total
        result["details"]["duration"] = duration
        result["details"]["tick"] = tick
        result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
        if reflected:
            result["details"]["reflected_to"] = attacker["name"]
        
        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === CHARM ===
    elif ability_type == "CHARM":
        if not target:
            result["error"] = "CHARM wymaga celu!"
            return result

        rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
        if rel_now == "ALLY":
            result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
            return result

        can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
        if not can:
            result["error"] = reason
            return result

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        # Etap 1: DVA
        roll_dva = random.randint(1, 100)
        dva_result = resolve_attack(attacker, target, slot, roll_dva, terrain_id)

        result["details"]["roll"] = roll_dva
        result["details"]["hit_chance"] = dva_result["hit_chance"]
        result["details"]["breakdown"] = dva_result["breakdown"]
        result["details"]["is_critical"] = dva_result["is_critical"]

        if not dva_result["success"]:
            result["success"] = False
            
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result

        # Etap 2: BAC (z formuły: 100/duration)
        duration = int(ability.get("duration", 1))
        duration = max(1, min(duration, 10))
        bac = 100.0 / float(duration)
        # SACRIFICE/EMPOWERED: podwojony BAC cap
        if ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice:
            bac = min(bac * 2.0, 100.0)
        if slot in ["B", "D"]:
            bac *= 0.5

        roll_bac = random.randint(1, 100)
        result["details"]["bac"] = bac
        result["details"]["roll_bac"] = roll_bac

        if roll_bac <= bac:
            # Blokuj duplikat CHARM po ability_name
            already_has = any(
                s.get("ability_name") == result["ability_name"]
                for s in target.get("statuses", [])
            )
            if already_has:
                result["success"] = False
                result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
                apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
                return result
            
            # REFLECT check
            reflected, reflect_log = try_reflect(target, attacker, "CHARM", result["details"], ability)
            result["details"]["reflect_log"] = reflect_log

            charm_victim = attacker if reflected else target
            charm_source = target["name"] if reflected else attacker["name"]

            if "statuses" not in charm_victim:
                charm_victim["statuses"] = []
            charm_victim["statuses"].append({
                "type": "CHARMED",
                "source": charm_source,
                "duration": duration,
                "bac": bac
            })
            result["success"] = True
            result["details"]["reflected"] = reflected
            result["details"]["status_applied"] = "CHARMED"
            result["details"]["duration"] = duration
            result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
            if reflected:
                result["details"]["reflected_to"] = attacker["name"]
        else:
            result["success"] = False
            result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === FEAR ===
    elif ability_type == "FEAR":
        if not target:
            result["error"] = "FEAR wymaga celu!"
            return result

        rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
        if rel_now == "ALLY":
            result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
            return result

        can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
        if not can:
            result["error"] = reason
            return result

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        # Etap 1: DVA
        roll_dva = random.randint(1, 100)
        dva_result = resolve_attack(attacker, target, slot, roll_dva, terrain_id)

        result["details"]["roll"] = roll_dva
        result["details"]["hit_chance"] = dva_result["hit_chance"]
        result["details"]["breakdown"] = dva_result["breakdown"]
        result["details"]["is_critical"] = dva_result["is_critical"]

        if not dva_result["success"]:
            result["success"] = False
            
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result

        # Etap 2: BAC (z formuły: 100/duration)
        duration = int(ability.get("duration", 1))
        duration = max(1, min(duration, 10))
        bac = 100.0 / float(duration)
        # SACRIFICE/EMPOWERED: podwojony BAC cap
        if ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice:
            bac = min(bac * 2.0, 100.0)
        if slot in ["B", "D"]:
            bac *= 0.5

        roll_bac = random.randint(1, 100)
        result["details"]["bac"] = bac
        result["details"]["roll_bac"] = roll_bac

        if roll_bac <= bac:
            # Blokuj duplikat FEAR po ability_name
            already_has = any(
                s.get("ability_name") == result["ability_name"]
                for s in target.get("statuses", [])
            )
            if already_has:
                result["success"] = False
                result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
                apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
                return result
            
            # REFLECT check
            reflected, reflect_log = try_reflect(target, attacker, "FEAR", result["details"], ability)
            result["details"]["reflect_log"] = reflect_log

            fear_victim = attacker if reflected else target
            fear_source = target["name"] if reflected else attacker["name"]

            if "statuses" not in fear_victim:
                fear_victim["statuses"] = []
            fear_victim["statuses"].append({
                "type": "FEARED",
                "source": fear_source,
                "duration": duration,
                "bac": bac
            })
            result["success"] = True
            result["details"]["reflected"] = reflected
            result["details"]["status_applied"] = "FEARED"
            result["details"]["duration"] = duration
            result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])
            if reflected:
                result["details"]["reflected_to"] = attacker["name"]
        else:
            result["success"] = False
            result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === TEMP_HP (tymczasowe punkty życia na self/ally) ===
    elif ability_type == "TEMP_HP":
        temp_target = target if target else attacker

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        # Nie można dawać TEMP_HP wrogowi
        if temp_target["name"] != attacker["name"]:
            rel = get_effective_relationship(attacker, temp_target)
            if rel == "ENEMY":
                result["error"] = "TEMP_HP można użyć tylko na siebie, sojusznika lub neutralnego."
                return result
            rel_change = handle_support_relationship(attacker, temp_target, all_units)
            result["details"]["rel_delta"] = rel_change["rel_delta"]
            result["details"]["rel_old"] = rel_change["rel_old"]
            result["details"]["rel_new"] = rel_change["rel_new"]
            result["details"]["new_allies_from_witness"] = rel_change["new_allies_from_witness"]

        value = float(ability.get("value", 0.75))
        duration = ability.get("duration", None)  # None = permanent
        ability_range = ability.get("range", 0)

        # Soft-cap
        if duration is None:
            cap = 0.75
        else:
            cap = 1.0

        if ability_range == 1:
            cap *= 0.75

        if ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice:
            cap *= 2.0

        if slot in ["B", "D"]:
            cap *= 0.5

        value = min(value, cap)

        # Blokuj duplikat po ability_name
        if "temp_hp_effects" not in temp_target:
            temp_target["temp_hp_effects"] = []
        already_has = any(
            e.get("ability_name") == result["ability_name"]
            for e in temp_target["temp_hp_effects"]
        )
        if already_has:
            result["success"] = False
            result["error"] = f"{temp_target['name']} już ma aktywny efekt {result['ability_name']}"
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result

        temp_target["temp_hp_effects"].append({
            "ability_name": result["ability_name"],
            "source": attacker["name"],
            "value": value,
            "duration": duration  # None = permanent
        })

        # Aplikuj do CON (temp_hp to suma BRACE + efektów)
        temp_target["temp_hp"] = float(temp_target.get("temp_hp", 0)) + value
        temp_target["con"] = float(temp_target["con"]) + value

        result["success"] = True
        result["target_name"] = temp_target["name"]
        result["details"]["temp_hp_gained"] = value
        result["details"]["total_temp_hp"] = float(temp_target["temp_hp"])
        result["details"]["duration"] = duration
        result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(temp_target["con"])

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === CLEANSE (usuwa negatywne efekty z ally/self) ===
    elif ability_type == "CLEANSE":
        cleanse_target = target if target else attacker

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        # Nie można cleansować wroga
        if cleanse_target["name"] != attacker["name"]:
            rel = get_effective_relationship(attacker, cleanse_target)
            if rel == "ENEMY":
                result["error"] = "CLEANSE można użyć tylko na siebie, sojusznika lub neutralnego."
                return result
            rel_change = handle_support_relationship(attacker, cleanse_target, all_units)
            result["details"]["rel_delta"] = rel_change["rel_delta"]
            result["details"]["rel_old"] = rel_change["rel_old"]
            result["details"]["rel_new"] = rel_change["rel_new"]
            result["details"]["new_allies_from_witness"] = rel_change["new_allies_from_witness"]

        # Zbierz co usuwamy
        removed = []

        # Negatywne statusy
        neg_statuses = ["STUN", "ROOT", "SILENCE", "DISARM", "CHARMED", "FEARED", "TAUNTED"]
        old_statuses = cleanse_target.get("statuses", [])
        kept_statuses = []
        for s in old_statuses:
            if s["type"] in neg_statuses:
                removed.append(f"status:{s['type']}")
            else:
                kept_statuses.append(s)
        cleanse_target["statuses"] = kept_statuses

        # Negatywne RES mody (VULN, WEAKEN_ATTACK)
        old_res = cleanse_target.get("res_modifiers", [])
        kept_res = []
        for m in old_res:
            if m.get("mode") in ["VULN", "WEAKEN_ATTACK"]:
                removed.append(f"res:{m.get('mode')}({m.get('ability_name','?')})")
            else:
                kept_res.append(m)
        cleanse_target["res_modifiers"] = kept_res

        # DOT / DRAIN_DOT
        old_ot = cleanse_target.get("ot_effects", [])
        kept_ot = []
        for e in old_ot:
            if e.get("type") in ["DOT", "DRAIN_DOT"]:
                removed.append(f"ot:{e.get('type')}({e.get('ability_name','?')})")
            else:
                kept_ot.append(e)
        cleanse_target["ot_effects"] = kept_ot

        result["success"] = True
        result["target_name"] = cleanse_target["name"]
        result["details"]["removed"] = removed
        result["details"]["removed_count"] = len(removed)
        result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(cleanse_target["con"])

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === DISPEL (usuwa pozytywne efekty z wroga) ===
    elif ability_type == "DISPEL":
        if not target:
            result["error"] = "DISPEL wymaga celu!"
            return result

        rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
        if rel_now == "ALLY":
            result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
            return result

        can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
        if not can:
            result["error"] = reason
            return result

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        # Zbierz co usuwamy
        removed = []

        # Pozytywne RES mody (SHIELD, PIERCE, RES_TEMP)
        old_res = target.get("res_modifiers", [])
        kept_res = []
        for m in old_res:
            if m.get("mode") in ["SHIELD", "PIERCE"] or m.get("type") == "RES_TEMP":
                removed.append(f"res:{m.get('mode', m.get('type','?'))}({m.get('ability_name','?')})")
            else:
                kept_res.append(m)
        target["res_modifiers"] = kept_res

        # HOT
        old_ot = target.get("ot_effects", [])
        kept_ot = []
        for e in old_ot:
            if e.get("type") == "HOT":
                removed.append(f"ot:HOT({e.get('ability_name','?')})")
            else:
                kept_ot.append(e)
        target["ot_effects"] = kept_ot

        # Pending heals
        old_ph = target.get("pending_heals", [])
        if old_ph:
            for p in old_ph:
                removed.append(f"pending_heal:{p.get('ability_name','?')}")
            target["pending_heals"] = []

        # TEMP_HP (BRACE + efekty zdolności)
        temp_hp = float(target.get("temp_hp", 0))
        if temp_hp > 0:
            target["con"] = float(target["con"]) - temp_hp
            target["temp_hp"] = 0
            removed.append(f"temp_hp:{temp_hp}")
        
        # Wyczyść trackowane efekty TEMP_HP
        for thp in target.get("temp_hp_effects", []):
            removed.append(f"temp_hp_effect:{thp.get('ability_name', '?')}")
        target["temp_hp_effects"] = []

        # Usuń REFLECT shields
        for rs in target.get("reflect_shields", []):
            removed.append(f"reflect:{rs.get('ability_name', '?')}")
        target["reflect_shields"] = []

        result["success"] = True
        result["details"]["removed"] = removed
        result["details"]["removed_count"] = len(removed)
        result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(target["con"])

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === REFLECT (lustrzana tarcza) ===
    elif ability_type == "REFLECT":
        reflect_target = target if target else attacker

        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        # Nie można dawać REFLECT wrogowi
        if reflect_target["name"] != attacker["name"]:
            rel = get_effective_relationship(attacker, reflect_target)
            if rel == "ENEMY":
                result["error"] = "REFLECT można użyć tylko na siebie, sojusznika lub neutralnego."
                return result
            rel_change = handle_support_relationship(attacker, reflect_target, all_units)
            result["details"]["rel_delta"] = rel_change["rel_delta"]
            result["details"]["rel_old"] = rel_change["rel_old"]
            result["details"]["rel_new"] = rel_change["rel_new"]
            result["details"]["new_allies_from_witness"] = rel_change["new_allies_from_witness"]

        slots = int(ability.get("slots", 1))
        bac_per_slot = 100.0 / float(slots)

        if "reflect_shields" not in reflect_target:
            reflect_target["reflect_shields"] = []

        # Zakaz stackowania tego samego REFLECT (po ability_name)
        already_has = any(
            rs.get("ability_name") == result["ability_name"] 
            for rs in reflect_target["reflect_shields"]
        )
        if already_has:
            result["success"] = False
            result["error"] = f"{reflect_target['name']} już ma aktywną tarczę {result['ability_name']}"
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result

        reflect_target["reflect_shields"].append({
            "ability_name": result["ability_name"],
            "source": attacker["name"],
            "slots": slots,
            "bac_per_slot": bac_per_slot
        })

        result["success"] = True
        result["target_name"] = reflect_target["name"]
        result["details"]["slots"] = slots
        result["details"]["bac_per_slot"] = bac_per_slot
        result["details"]["target_con_after"] = float(reflect_target["con"])

        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === COOLDOWN MANIPULATION (BURN / REFRESH) ===
    elif ability_type in ["COOLDOWN_BURN", "COOLDOWN_REFRESH"]:
        is_burn = (ability_type == "COOLDOWN_BURN")
        
        if is_burn:
            if not target:
                result["error"] = "BURN wymaga celu!"
                return result
            rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
            if rel_now == "ALLY":
                result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
                return result
            can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
            if not can:
                result["error"] = reason
                return result
        else:
            heal_target = target if target else attacker
            target = heal_target # target needed for distance check
        
        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result

        if is_burn:
            roll = random.randint(1, 100)
            dva_result = resolve_attack(attacker, target, slot, roll, terrain_id)
            
            result["details"]["roll"] = roll
            result["details"]["hit_chance"] = dva_result["hit_chance"]
            result["details"]["breakdown"] = dva_result["breakdown"]
            
            if not dva_result["success"]:
                result["success"] = False
                
                apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
                return result
        else:
            result["success"] = True

        value = int(ability.get("value", 1))
        value_cap = 2 if slot in ["A", "C"] else 1
        if ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice:
            value_cap *= 2
        value = min(value, value_cap)
        affected_slots = []
        
        # REFLECT check (only for BURN)
        if is_burn:
            reflected, reflect_log = try_reflect(target, attacker, ability_type, result["details"], ability)
            result["details"]["reflect_log"] = reflect_log
            result["details"]["reflected"] = reflected
            cd_victim = attacker if reflected else target
            if reflected:
                result["details"]["reflected_to"] = attacker["name"]
        else:
            reflected = False
            cd_victim = target
        
        for s in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]:
            if is_burn:
                cd_victim["cooldowns"][s] += value
                affected_slots.append(f"{s}(+{value})")
            else:
                if cd_victim["cooldowns"][s] > 0:
                    cd_victim["cooldowns"][s] = max(0, cd_victim["cooldowns"][s] - value)
                    affected_slots.append(f"{s}(-{value})")
        
        result["success"] = True
        result["details"]["affected_slots"] = ", ".join(affected_slots)
        
        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # === TAUNT (Wymuszenie ataku) ===
    elif ability_type == "TAUNT":
        if not target:
            result["error"] = "TAUNT wymaga celu!"
            return result

        rel_now = get_effective_relationship(attacker, target)
        if rel_now == "ALLY":
            result["error"] = "Cel jest teraz sojusznikiem — atak anulowany"
            return result
            
        can, reason = can_attack(attacker, target)
        if not can:
            result["error"] = reason
            return result
            
        if not _check_range():
            result["error"] = "Cel poza zasięgiem"
            return result
            
        # Etap 1: DVA
        roll_dva = random.randint(1, 100)
        dva_result = resolve_attack(attacker, target, slot, roll_dva, terrain_id)
        
        result["details"]["roll"] = roll_dva
        result["details"]["hit_chance"] = dva_result["hit_chance"]
        result["details"]["breakdown"] = dva_result["breakdown"]
        
        if not dva_result["success"]:
            result["success"] = False
            
            apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
            return result
            
        # Etap 2: BAC
        bac = float(ability.get("bac", 100))
        bac_cap = 200.0 if (ability.get("empowered") or is_sacrifice) else 100.0
        bac = min(bac, bac_cap)
        if slot in ["B", "D"]:
            bac *= 0.5
            
        roll_bac = random.randint(1, 100)
        result["details"]["bac"] = bac
        result["details"]["roll_bac"] = roll_bac
        
        if roll_bac <= bac:
            duration = int(ability.get("duration", 2))
            
            # Blokuj duplikat TAUNT po ability_name
            already_has = any(
                s.get("ability_name") == result["ability_name"]
                for s in target.get("statuses", [])
            )
            if already_has:
                result["success"] = False
                apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
                return result
            
            # REFLECT check
            reflected, reflect_log = try_reflect(target, attacker, "TAUNT", result["details"], ability)
            result["details"]["reflect_log"] = reflect_log

            taunt_victim = attacker if reflected else target
            taunt_source = target["name"] if reflected else attacker["name"]

            if "statuses" not in taunt_victim: taunt_victim["statuses"] = []
            taunt_victim["statuses"].append({
                "type": "TAUNTED",
                "source": taunt_source,
                "duration": duration
            })
            
            result["success"] = True
            result["details"]["reflected"] = reflected
            result["details"]["status_applied"] = "TAUNTED"
            result["details"]["duration"] = duration
            if reflected:
                result["details"]["reflected_to"] = attacker["name"]
        else:
            result["success"] = False
            
        apply_cooldown(attacker, slot)
        return result

    # Zeruj setup_stacks po użyciu zdolności EMPOWERED (niezależnie od wyniku)
    # SACRIFICE nie używa stacków — nie resetuj
    if ability.get("empowered", False) and not is_sacrifice:
        attacker["setup_stacks"] = 0
        result["details"]["empowered_reset"] = True
    
    return result

def r2(val):
    """Zaokrągla float do 2 miejsc po przecinku."""
    try:
        return round(float(val), 2)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return val


# === SYSTEM ARGOS (Kolejność aktywacji) ===

def select_next_unit():
    """
    Wybiera następną jednostkę do aktywacji według algorytmu Argos.
    Im dłużej jednostka czeka, tym większa szansa na aktywację.
    """
    world = sm.get_world()
    if not world or not world["units"]:
        return None
    
    units = list(world["units"].values())
    
    # Filtruj tylko żywe jednostki
    alive_units = [u for u in units if u["con"] > 0]
    if not alive_units:
        return None
    
    for attempt in range(100):
        candidate = random.choice(alive_units)
        n = candidate.get("turns_since_action", 0) + 1
        prob = min(1.0, (n * (n + 1)) / 100.0)
        
        roll = random.random()
        if roll < prob:
            return candidate
    
    return max(alive_units, key=lambda u: u.get("turns_since_action", 0))


def select_next_unit_with_log():
    """
    Wybiera jednostkę z pełnym logowaniem do audytu.
    """
    
    world = sm.get_world()
    if not world or not world["units"]:
        return None
    
    units = list(world["units"].values())
    alive_units = [u for u in units if u["con"] > 0]
    if not alive_units:
        return None
    
    log_to_audit("\n--- LOSOWANIE INICJATYWY ---")
    
    for attempt in range(100):
        candidate = random.choice(alive_units)
        n = candidate.get("turns_since_action", 0) + 1
        prob = min(1.0, (n * (n + 1)) / 100.0)
        roll = random.random()
        
        result = "SUKCES" if roll < prob else "PORAŻKA"
        log_to_audit(f"Próba {attempt + 1}: {candidate['name']} (n={n}, szansa={prob*100:.1f}%, rzut={roll*100:.1f}%) -> {result}")
        
        if roll < prob:
            log_to_audit(f"\n>>> WYLOSOWANO: {candidate['name']} <<<\n")
            return candidate
    
    # Fallback
    fallback = max(alive_units, key=lambda u: u.get("turns_since_action", 0))
    log_to_audit(f"\n>>> FALLBACK (max oczekiwania): {fallback['name']} <<<\n")
    return fallback


# === GŁÓWNA PĘTLA TURY ===

def distribute_death_renown(dead_unit, world):
    """
    Rozdziela 50% renomy martwej jednostki równo między jej wrogów.
    Loguje do audytu i UI.
    """
    from domains import get_effective_relationship
    
    dead_name = dead_unit["name"]
    dead_renown = float(dead_unit.get("renown", 0))
    pool = round(dead_renown * 0.5, 2)
    
    if pool <= 0:
        return
    
    # Znajdź żywych wrogów
    enemies = []
    for u in world["units"].values():
        if u["name"] == dead_name:
            continue
        if float(u["con"]) <= 0:
            continue
        rel = get_effective_relationship(u, dead_unit)
        if rel == "ENEMY":
            enemies.append(u)
    
    if not enemies:
        log_to_audit(f"  RENOMA: {dead_name} miał {r2(dead_renown)} renomy, brak żywych wrogów do rozdzielenia")
        world["log"].append(f"  🏆 {dead_name}: {r2(dead_renown)} renomy, brak wrogów do rozdzielenia")
        return
    
    share = round(pool / len(enemies), 4)
    
    log_to_audit(f"  RENOMA ŚMIERCI: {dead_name} ({r2(dead_renown)}) → {r2(pool)} rozdzielone między {len(enemies)} wrogów ({r2(share)} każdy)")
    world["log"].append(f"  🏆 {dead_name}: {r2(pool)} renomy rozdzielone między {len(enemies)} wrogów")
    
    for enemy in enemies:
        enemy["renown"] = round(float(enemy.get("renown", 0)) + share, 4)
        log_to_audit(f"    {enemy['name']}: +{r2(share)} → {r2(enemy['renown'])}")
        world["log"].append(f"    🏆 {enemy['name']}: +{r2(share)} renomy")


def run_turn_cycle():
    """
    Główna pętla tury:
    1. Wybierz jednostkę (Argos)
    2. Tick cooldownów i statusów
    3. AI decyduje o 3 akcjach
    4. Wykonaj akcje
    5. Zwróć wyniki dla Weryfikatora
    """
    world = sm.get_world()
    if not world or not world["units"]:
        return None
    
    # Inkrementuj turę
    world["turn"] += 1
    world["log"] = [f"--- TURA {world['turn']} ---"]
    
    # Wybierz jednostkę (z logowaniem)
    unit = select_next_unit_with_log()
    if not unit:
        return None
        
    # Log do pliku (teraz zmienna unit już istnieje)
    log_to_audit(f"\n--- TURA {world['turn']}: {unit['name']} ---")
    
    unit_name = unit["name"]
    world["log"].append(f"🎯 Aktywacja: **{unit_name}**")
    
    # Zapamiętaj cooldowny sprzed aktywacji (żeby tickować tylko te)
    cooldowns_at_start = {slot: unit["cooldowns"].get(slot, 0) for slot in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]}

    # === NAGRODA ZA RENOMĘ (Leczenie za każdy pełny punkt) ===
    current_milestone = int(unit.get("renown", 0))
    last_milestone = unit.get("last_renown_milestone", 0)
    
    if current_milestone > last_milestone:
        points_gained = current_milestone - last_milestone
        heal_amount = points_gained * 0.5
        
        old_con = float(unit["con"])
        unit["con"] = min(float(unit["max_con"]), old_con + heal_amount)
        actual_heal = round(float(unit["con"]) - old_con, 2)
        
        unit["last_renown_milestone"] = current_milestone
        
        if actual_heal > 0:
            world["log"].append(f"  🏆 FAVOR: +{r2(actual_heal)} HP (milestone {current_milestone})")
            log_to_audit(f"  FAVOR: +{r2(actual_heal)} HP (milestone {current_milestone}, renoma: {r2(unit['renown'])})")
        else:
            world["log"].append(f"  🏆 FAVOR: milestone {current_milestone} (pełne HP, brak leczenia)")
            log_to_audit(f"  FAVOR: milestone {current_milestone} (pełne HP, renoma: {r2(unit['renown'])})")

    # === ENTROPIA: obrażenia za bezczynność (zjada najpierw TEMP_HP) ===
    # Inkrementuj entropię WSZYSTKICH (entropia = upływ czasu, nie czekanie)
    for u in world["units"].values():
        u["entropy_counter"] = u.get("entropy_counter", 0) + 1
    
    # === HOT ticki (PRZED entropią — regeneracja zanim entropia zjada) ===
    # Wyciągnij HOT z ot_effects osobno, DOT/DRAIN po entropii
    hot_events = []
    hot_renown = []
    hot_expired = []
    non_hot_effects = []
    
    for eff in unit.get("ot_effects", []):
        if eff.get("type") == "HOT":
            eff["duration"] -= 1
            tick = float(eff.get("tick", 0.0))
            source = eff.get("source")
            
            temp_hp = float(unit.get("temp_hp", 0))
            real_con = float(unit["con"]) - temp_hp
            max_con = float(unit["max_con"])
            
            actual_heal = min(tick, max_con - real_con)
            actual_heal = max(0.0, actual_heal)
            
            unit["con"] = float(unit["con"]) + actual_heal
            hot_events.append(f"HOT tick {eff.get('ability_name','?')}: +{round(actual_heal, 4)}")
            if source and actual_heal > 0:
                hot_renown.append({"source": source, "amount": actual_heal})
            
            if eff["duration"] <= 0:
                hot_expired.append(eff)
            else:
                non_hot_effects.append(eff)
        else:
            non_hot_effects.append(eff)
    
    unit["ot_effects"] = non_hot_effects
    
    for ev in hot_events:
        world["log"].append(f"  ⏳ {ev}")
        log_to_audit(f"  {ev}")
    for re in hot_renown:
        src = world["units"].get(re["source"])
        if src:
            src["renown"] = round(float(src.get("renown", 0)) + re["amount"], 4)

    # === ENTROPIA (po HOT, zjada BRACE → efekty TEMP_HP → realne HP) ===
    from turn_ticks import _absorb_damage_through_temp
    
    idle_turns = unit.get("entropy_counter", 0)
    if idle_turns > 0:
        entropy_damage = round(idle_turns * 0.1, 2)
        brace_abs, eff_abs, real_dmg = _absorb_damage_through_temp(unit, entropy_damage)
        total_absorbed = round(brace_abs + eff_abs, 4)
        
        world["log"].append(f"  ⏳ ENTROPIA: -{r2(entropy_damage)} ({idle_turns} akt × 0.1)")
        if total_absorbed > 0:
            absorb_parts = []
            if brace_abs > 0:
                absorb_parts.append(f"BRACE: {r2(brace_abs)}")
            if eff_abs > 0:
                absorb_parts.append(f"efekty: {r2(eff_abs)}")
            log_to_audit(f"  ENTROPIA: -{r2(entropy_damage)} ({idle_turns} akt × 0.1) — pochłonięto {r2(total_absorbed)} ({', '.join(absorb_parts)}), realne obrażenia: {r2(real_dmg)}")
        else:
            log_to_audit(f"  ENTROPIA: -{r2(entropy_damage)} ({idle_turns} akt × 0.1)")

    # === DOT/DRAIN_DOT ticki (PO entropii, zjadają TEMP_HP tak jak entropia) ===
    ot_events, ot_expired, drain_heals, ot_renown = tick_ot_effects(unit)
    for ev in ot_events:
        world["log"].append(f"  ⏳ {ev}")
        log_to_audit(f"  {ev}")

    # === BRACE wygasa (po DOT — DOT może zjeść BRACE zanim wygaśnie) ===
    total_temp = float(unit.get("temp_hp", 0))
    effects_temp = sum(float(e["value"]) for e in unit.get("temp_hp_effects", []))
    brace_remaining = max(0.0, total_temp - effects_temp)
    if brace_remaining > 0:
        unit["con"] = max(0.0, float(unit["con"]) - brace_remaining)
        unit["temp_hp"] = max(0.0, total_temp - brace_remaining)
        world["log"].append(f"  🛡️ BRACE TEMP_HP {r2(brace_remaining)} wygasa")
        log_to_audit(f"  BRACE TEMP_HP EXPIRED: {r2(brace_remaining)}")

    # === TEMP_HP efekty tick duration (wygaś te z duration=0) ===
    expired_temp_effects, temp_removed = tick_temp_hp_effects(unit)
    for eff in expired_temp_effects:
        world["log"].append(f"  🛡️ TEMP_HP {eff.get('ability_name','?')} ({r2(eff['value'])}) wygasa (czas)")
        log_to_audit(f"  TEMP_HP EFFECT EXPIRED: {eff.get('ability_name','?')} ({r2(eff['value'])})")

    # Tick CHARMED/FEARED (BAC recheck)
    special_effects, special_remove_after, special_logs = tick_special_statuses(unit)
    for sl in special_logs:
        world["log"].append(f"  🎭 {sl}")
        log_to_audit(f"  SPECIAL: {sl}")

    # Przetwórz DRAIN_DOT heale na źródłach
    for dh in drain_heals:
        source = world["units"].get(dh["source"])
        if source:
            source_temp = float(source.get("temp_hp", 0))
            source_real = float(source["con"]) - source_temp
            source_max = float(source["max_con"])
            
            actual = min(dh["amount"], source_max - source_real)
            actual = max(0.0, actual)
            
            source["con"] = float(source["con"]) + actual
            world["log"].append(f"  🧛 DRAIN: {dh['source']} leczy się za {r2(actual)} ({dh['ability_name']})")
            log_to_audit(f"  DRAIN_HEAL: {dh['source']} +{r2(actual)} (from {unit_name}, {dh['ability_name']})")
            if actual > 0:
                ot_renown.append({"source": dh["source"], "amount": actual})

    # Renoma z ticków (DOT/DRAIN)
    for re in ot_renown:
        src = world["units"].get(re["source"])
        if src:
            src["renown"] = round(float(src.get("renown", 0)) + re["amount"], 4)

    # Tick pending heals (delay na casterze)
    ph_events, ph_renown = tick_pending_heals(unit, world["units"])
    for ev in ph_events:
        world["log"].append(f"  ✨ {ev}")
        log_to_audit(f"  {ev}")

    # Renoma z pending heals
    for re in ph_renown:
        src = world["units"].get(re["source"])
        if src:
            src["renown"] = round(float(src.get("renown", 0)) + re["amount"], 4)

    # === SPRAWDZENIE ŚMIERCI (po entropii + DOT + wygaśnięciu TEMP_HP) ===
    if float(unit["con"]) <= 0:
        # Określ przyczynę śmierci
        if idle_turns > 0 and entropy_damage > 0:
            death_cause = "ENTROPIA"
            death_msg = "umiera z entropii"
        else:
            death_cause = "DOT"
            death_msg = "umiera z obrażeń okresowych"
        
        world["log"].append(f"  💀 {unit['name']} {death_msg}!")
        log_to_audit(f"  >>> {unit['name']} {death_msg.upper()} <<<")
        
        distribute_death_renown(unit, world)
        
        for other in world["units"].values():
            if other["name"] != unit_name:
                other["turns_since_action"] = other.get("turns_since_action", 0) + 1
                other["entropy_counter"] = other.get("entropy_counter", 0) + 1
        
        sm.save_game_state()
        return {
            "unit": unit,
            "actions": [],
            "action_results": [],
            "combat_occurred": True,
            "dead_units": [unit["name"]],
            "start_position": unit["position"].copy(),
            "con_after_ticks": float(unit["con"]),
            "death_cause": death_cause
        }
    
    # Aktualizuj liczniki Argos
    unit["turns_since_action"] = 0
    unit["entropy_counter"] = 0  # Reset po odbyciu aktywacji
    unit["last_turn_index"] = world["turn"]
    
    for other in world["units"].values():
        if other["name"] != unit_name:
            other["turns_since_action"] = other.get("turns_since_action", 0) + 1
    
    # Zapamiętaj stan po tickach (przed akcjami) — do promptu Opisywacza i logu
    con_after_ticks = float(unit["con"])
    temp_hp_now = float(unit.get("temp_hp", 0))
    real_hp = round(con_after_ticks - temp_hp_now, 2)
    
    if temp_hp_now > 0:
        log_to_audit(f"  STAN: {r2(real_hp)} + {r2(temp_hp_now)} TEMP = {r2(con_after_ticks)}/{r2(unit['max_con'])}")
    else:
        log_to_audit(f"  STAN: {r2(con_after_ticks)}/{r2(unit['max_con'])}")
    
    # Zapamiętaj pozycję startową (przed ruchami)
    start_position = unit["position"].copy()
    
    all_units = list(world["units"].values())
    
    # Snapshot widocznych jednostek na starcie (przed akcjami, z aktualnym CON)
    start_visible = {}
    for u in all_units:
        if u["name"] != unit_name and u["con"] > 0:
            start_visible[u["name"]] = {
                "name": u["name"],
                "position": u["position"].copy(),
                "con": float(u["con"]),
                "max_con": float(u["max_con"]),
                "domains": u["domains"].copy()
            }
    
    # Wykonaj akcje i zbierz wyniki (inicjalizacja przed rozproszeniem)
    action_results = []
    combat_occurred = False
    shared_field = False
    
    # Sprawdź shared_field na starcie
    others_here = [u for u in all_units if u["name"] != unit_name and u["position"] == unit["position"] and u["con"] > 0]
    if others_here:
        shared_field = True

    # === ROZPROSZENIE: jeśli ≥4 jednostek na polu, 3 wymuszone ruchy w losowym kierunku ===
    has_stun = any(s["type"] == "STUN" for s in unit.get("statuses", []))
    has_root = any(s["type"] == "ROOT" for s in unit.get("statuses", []))
    
    if not has_stun and not has_root:
        others_on_field = [u for u in all_units if u["name"] != unit_name and u["position"] == unit["position"] and u["con"] > 0]
        
        # Tłok (≥4 na polu): 100% rozproszenie
        # Brak tłoku ale ≥1 osoba: 20% szans na bonusowy sprint
        do_scatter = False
        scatter_reason = ""
        
        if len(others_on_field) >= 3:
            do_scatter = True
            scatter_reason = f"TŁOK: {len(others_on_field)+1} jednostek na polu"
        elif len(others_on_field) >= 1 and random.random() < 0.20:
            do_scatter = True
            scatter_reason = f"SPRINT: {len(others_on_field)+1} jednostek na polu, losowy zryw"
        
        if do_scatter:
            scatter_dir = random.choice(["N", "NE", "E", "SE", "S", "SW", "W", "NW"])
            world["log"].append(f"  👥 {scatter_reason} — rozproszenie {scatter_dir}")
            log_to_audit(f"  {scatter_reason}, rozproszenie kierunek {scatter_dir}")
            
            for scatter_step in range(3):
                scatter_result = execute_move(unit, scatter_dir, world["game_map"])
                action_results.append({
                    "type": "MOVE",
                    "direction": scatter_dir,
                    "result": scatter_result
                })
                
                if scatter_result.get("success"):
                    terrain_id = scatter_result.get("terrain")
                    terrain_name = BIOMES.get(terrain_id, {}).get("name", "?") if terrain_id else "?"
                    log_to_audit(f"\nROZPROSZENIE {scatter_step+1}: RUCH {scatter_dir} -> {terrain_name}")
                    
                    # Sprawdź shared_field po ruchu
                    if not shared_field:
                        others_here = [u for u in all_units if u["name"] != unit_name and u["position"] == unit["position"] and u["con"] > 0]
                        if others_here:
                            shared_field = True
                else:
                    log_to_audit(f"\nROZPROSZENIE {scatter_step+1}: RUCH {scatter_dir} -> ZABLOKOWANY (krawędź mapy)")
                    break
            
    # FEARED override: 3× ruch od źródła strachu
    
    if "FEARED" in special_effects and not has_stun and not has_root:
        fear_source_name = special_effects["FEARED"].get("source")
        fear_source = world["units"].get(fear_source_name)
        
        if fear_source:
            actions = []
            flee_pos = unit["position"].copy()
            
            for _ in range(3):
                dx = flee_pos[0] - fear_source["position"][0]
                dy = flee_pos[1] - fear_source["position"][1]
                
                if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
                    direction = random.choice(["N", "NE", "E", "SE", "S", "SW", "W", "NW"])
                else:
                    dx = max(-1, min(1, dx))
                    dy = max(-1, min(1, dy))
                    dir_map = {
                        (0,1):"N", (1,1):"NE", (1,0):"E", (1,-1):"SE",
                        (0,-1):"S", (-1,-1):"SW", (-1,0):"W", (-1,1):"NW"
                    }
                    direction = dir_map.get((dx, dy), "N")
                
                actions.append({"type": "MOVE", "direction": direction})
                
                ddx, ddy = direction_to_delta(direction)
                flee_pos = [flee_pos[0] + ddx, flee_pos[1] + ddy]
            
            world["log"].append(f"  😱 {unit_name} ucieka w panice!")
            log_to_audit(f"  FEARED: {unit_name} ucieka od {fear_source_name}")
        else:
            # Źródło strachu nie żyje — strach znika
            unit["statuses"] = [s for s in unit["statuses"] if s["type"] != "FEARED"]
            world["log"].append(f"  😱 Źródło strachu nie żyje — {unit_name} uwalnia się!")
            log_to_audit(f"  FEARED: source {fear_source_name} dead — fear breaks")
            actions = []  # Normalne planowanie krok po kroku
    else:
        # Normalne AI — planowanie krok po kroku (interleave z wykonaniem)
        actions = []
    
    # Dla FEARED: akcje już zaplanowane; dla normalnego AI: planuj po kolei
    is_feared = "FEARED" in special_effects
    used_abilities = set()
    current_pos = unit["position"].copy()
    
    for action_idx in range(3):
        action_num = action_idx + 1
        
        # Planuj następną akcję (chyba że FEARED — wtedy z gotowej listy)
        if is_feared:
            if action_idx < len(actions):
                action = actions[action_idx]
            else:
                break
        else:
            result_tuple = decide_single_action(
                unit, all_units, world["game_map"], current_pos, used_abilities
            )
            if result_tuple is None or result_tuple[0] is None:
                # Loguj drzewko decyzyjne nawet gdy brak akcji
                if result_tuple and result_tuple[1]:
                    decision_log = " → ".join(result_tuple[1])
                    world["log"].append(f"  🧠 Akcja {action_num}: {decision_log} → BRAK")
                continue
            action, decision_log = result_tuple
            
            # Loguj drzewko decyzyjne do UI
            if decision_log:
                world["log"].append(f"  🧠 Akcja {action_num}: {' → '.join(decision_log)}")
        
        if action["type"] == "MOVE":
            result = execute_move(unit, action["direction"], world["game_map"])
            action_results.append({
                "type": "MOVE",
                "direction": action["direction"],
                "result": result
            })
            world["log"].append(f"  ➡️ Ruch: {action['direction']}")
            
            # Sprawdź czy po ruchu jesteśmy z kimś na polu
            if result.get("success") and not shared_field:
                others_here = [u for u in all_units if u["name"] != unit_name and u["position"] == unit["position"] and u["con"] > 0]
                if others_here:
                    shared_field = True
            
            # Log do audytu
            if result.get("success"):
                terrain_id = result.get("terrain")
                terrain_name = BIOMES.get(terrain_id, {}).get("name", "?") if terrain_id else "?"
                log_to_audit(f"\nAKCJA {action_num}: RUCH {action['direction']} -> {terrain_name}")
            else:
                log_to_audit(f"\nAKCJA {action_num}: RUCH {action['direction']} -> ZABLOKOWANY ({result.get('error', '?')})")
        
        elif action["type"] == "ABILITY":
            slot = action.get("slot")
            target_name = action.get("target")
            
            if not slot:
                # AI nie wybrało slotu - pomiń
                action_results.append({
                    "type": "ABILITY",
                    "slot": None,
                    "target": target_name,
                    "result": {"success": None, "error": "AI nie wybrało zdolności"}
                })
                continue
            
            result = execute_ability(
                unit, 
                slot, 
                target_name,
                all_units,
                world["game_map"]
            )
            action_results.append({
                "type": "ABILITY",
                "slot": slot,
                "target": target_name,
                "result": result
            })
            
            if result.get("success") is not None and target_name:
                combat_occurred = True
            
            # Renoma za akcję (oblicz teraz, loguj na końcu bloku)
            action_renown = calculate_renown_from_result(result)
            if action_renown > 0:
                unit["renown"] = round(float(unit.get("renown", 0)) + action_renown, 4)
            
            # REFLECT: renoma idzie do właściciela tarczy, nie do atakującego
            if result.get("details", {}).get("reflected"):
                reflect_log = result["details"].get("reflect_log", {})
                reflect_source = reflect_log.get("shield_source")
                if reflect_source:
                    reflect_owner = sm.get_unit(reflect_source)
                    if reflect_owner:
                        # Oblicz renomę jakby obrońca zadał te obrażenia
                        reflect_renown = 0.0
                        r_details = result.get("details", {})
                        r_type = result.get("ability_type", "")
                        if r_type in ["CON_DMG", "MELEE"]:
                            reflect_renown = float(r_details.get("damage", 0))
                        elif r_type == "DRAINLIFE":
                            reflect_renown = float(r_details.get("damage", 0)) + float(r_details.get("heal_amount", 0))
                        elif r_type in ["ROOT", "STUN", "SILENCE", "DISARM", "CHARM", "FEAR"]:
                            reflect_renown = 0.2
                        elif r_type in ["DOT", "DRAINLIFE_DOT"]:
                            reflect_renown = 0.2  # Bonus za nałożenie odbite
                        elif isinstance(r_type, str) and r_type.startswith("RES_") and ("VULN" in r_type or "WEAKEN" in r_type):
                            reflect_renown = 0.2
                        
                        if reflect_renown > 0:
                            reflect_owner["renown"] = round(float(reflect_owner.get("renown", 0)) + reflect_renown, 4)
                            log_to_audit(f"  REFLECT RENOMA +{r2(reflect_renown)} -> {reflect_source} (łącznie: {r2(reflect_owner['renown'])})")
            
            success_str = "✅" if result.get("success") else "❌"
            ability_type = result.get("ability_type", "")
            
            # AOE: pokaż wszystkie trafione cele
            if ability_type == "AOE" and result.get("success"):
                hit_results = result.get("details", {}).get("hit_results", [])
                hit_names = [hr["name"] for hr in hit_results if hr.get("bac_success") or hr.get("dot_applied") or hr.get("hot_applied") or hr.get("res_applied") or hr.get("status_applied")]
                if hit_names:
                    world["log"].append(f"  ⚔️ {slot}: {result.get('ability_name', '?')} → {', '.join(hit_names)} {success_str}")
                else:
                    world["log"].append(f"  ⚔️ {slot}: {result.get('ability_name', '?')} → {target_name} {success_str}")
            else:
                world["log"].append(f"  ⚔️ {slot}: {result.get('ability_name', '?')} → {target_name} {success_str}")
            
            # Log do audytu
            ability_name = result.get("ability_name", "?")
            ability_type = result.get("ability_type", "?")
            details = result.get("details", {})
            
            log_to_audit(f"\nAKCJA {action_num}: {ability_name} ({ability_type})")
            log_to_audit(f"  Cel: {target_name}")
            
            # Log SACRIFICE
            if details.get("sacrifice_cost"):
                sac_cost = r2(details["sacrifice_cost"])
                sac_con = r2(details["attacker_con_after_sacrifice"])
                log_to_audit(f"  SACRIFICE: -{sac_cost} HP → {unit_name}: {sac_con} CON")
                if details.get("sacrifice_killed_caster"):
                    log_to_audit(f"  >>> {unit_name} POŚWIĘCA SIĘ (śmierć z SACRIFICE) <<<")
            
            if result.get("success") is None:
                log_to_audit(f"  Wynik: BŁĄD - {result.get('error', '?')}")
            else:
                # --- DVA BREAKDOWN (jeśli jest) ---
                if "breakdown" in details and details["breakdown"]:
                    log_to_audit(details["breakdown"])
                
                # --- Wynik CC (dwuetapowy: DVA + BAC) ---
                if ability_type in ["ROOT", "STUN", "SILENCE", "DISARM", "CHARM", "FEAR", "TAUNT"]:
                    crit = " (KRYTYK!)" if details.get("is_critical") else ""
                    dva_hit = details.get("roll", 0) <= details.get("hit_chance", 0) or details.get("is_critical", False)
                    log_to_audit(f"  Rzut DVA: {details.get('roll', '?')}{crit} → {'TRAFIENIE' if dva_hit else 'PUDŁO'}")
                    if dva_hit and "roll_bac" in details:
                        log_to_audit(f"  Rzut BAC: {details.get('roll_bac')} vs {r2(details.get('bac','?'))}%")
                    if result.get("success"):
                        status = details.get("status_applied", ability_type)
                        dur = details.get("duration", "?")
                        if details.get("reflected"):
                            rlog = details.get("reflect_log", {})
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> SUKCES ale ODBITE przez {rlog.get('shield_name','?')} (BAC {rlog.get('roll','?')} vs {r2(rlog.get('bac','?'))}%): {status} na {dur} aktywacji NAŁOŻONE NA ATAKUJĄCEGO ({unit_name}) <<<")
                        else:
                            reflect_log = details.get("reflect_log")
                            if reflect_log and not reflect_log.get("reflected"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  REFLECT próba: {reflect_log.get('shield_name','?')} BAC {reflect_log.get('roll','?')} vs {r2(reflect_log.get('bac','?'))}% — nie odbito")
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> SUKCES: {status} na {dur} aktywacji <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")
                
                # --- Wynik DMG ---
                elif ability_type == "CON_DMG":
                    crit = " (KRYTYK!)" if details.get("is_critical") else ""
                    log_to_audit(f"  Rzut: {details.get('roll', '?')}{crit}")
                    if result.get("success"):
                        if details.get("reflected"):
                            rlog = details.get("reflect_log", {})
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE ale ODBITE przez {rlog.get('shield_name','?')} (BAC {rlog.get('roll','?')} vs {r2(rlog.get('bac','?'))}%): {r2(details.get('damage', 0))} DMG na atakującego — {unit_name}: {r2(details.get('attacker_con_after', '?'))} CON <<<")
                            if details.get("attacker_eliminated"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  >>> {unit_name} ZABITY WŁASNYM ATAKIEM (REFLECT) <<<")
                        else:
                            reflect_log = details.get("reflect_log")
                            if reflect_log and not reflect_log.get("reflected"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  REFLECT próba: {reflect_log.get('shield_name','?')} BAC {reflect_log.get('roll','?')} vs {r2(reflect_log.get('bac','?'))}% — nie odbito")
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE: {r2(details.get('damage', 0))} DMG — cel: {r2(details.get('target_con_after', '?'))} CON <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")

                # --- DRAINLIFE ---
                elif ability_type == "DRAINLIFE":
                    crit = " (KRYTYK!)" if details.get("is_critical") else ""
                    log_to_audit(f"  Rzut: {details.get('roll', '?')}{crit}")
                    if result.get("success"):
                        if details.get("reflected"):
                            rlog = details.get("reflect_log", {})
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE ale ODBITE przez {rlog.get('shield_name','?')}: {r2(details.get('damage', 0))} DMG na atakującego, +{r2(details.get('heal_amount', 0))} HEAL dla celu — {unit_name}: {r2(details.get('attacker_con_after', '?'))} CON <<<")
                            if details.get("attacker_eliminated"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  >>> {unit_name} ZABITY WŁASNYM ATAKIEM (REFLECT) <<<")
                        else:
                            reflect_log = details.get("reflect_log")
                            if reflect_log and not reflect_log.get("reflected"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  REFLECT próba: {reflect_log.get('shield_name','?')} BAC {reflect_log.get('roll','?')} vs {r2(reflect_log.get('bac','?'))}% — nie odbito")
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE: {r2(details.get('damage', 0))} DMG, +{r2(details.get('heal_amount', 0))} HEAL — cel: {r2(details.get('target_con_after', '?'))} CON <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")

                # --- DRAINLIFE_DOT ---
                elif ability_type == "DRAINLIFE_DOT":
                    crit = " (KRYTYK!)" if details.get("is_critical") else ""
                    log_to_audit(f"  Rzut: {details.get('roll', '?')}{crit}")
                    if result.get("success"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE: DRAIN_DOT {r2(details.get('tick', '?'))}/akt x{details.get('duration', '?')} <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")

                # --- DOT ---
                elif ability_type == "DOT":
                    crit = " (KRYTYK!)" if details.get("is_critical") else ""
                    log_to_audit(f"  Rzut: {details.get('roll', '?')}{crit}")
                    if result.get("success"):
                        if details.get("reflected"):
                            rlog = details.get("reflect_log", {})
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE ale ODBITE przez {rlog.get('shield_name','?')}: DOT {r2(details.get('tick', '?'))}/akt x{details.get('duration', '?')} NAŁOŻONY NA ATAKUJĄCEGO ({unit_name}) <<<")
                        else:
                            reflect_log = details.get("reflect_log")
                            if reflect_log and not reflect_log.get("reflected"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  REFLECT próba: {reflect_log.get('shield_name','?')} BAC {reflect_log.get('roll','?')} vs {r2(reflect_log.get('bac','?'))}% — nie odbito")
                            log_to_audit(f"  >>> TRAFIENIE: DOT {r2(details.get('tick', '?'))}/akt x{details.get('duration', '?')} <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")
                
                # --- HEAL ---
                elif ability_type == "HEAL":
                    if details.get("scheduled"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> ZAPLANOWANE: {r2(details.get('heal_amount', '?'))} HEAL za {details.get('delay', '?')} aktywacji (BAC: {r2(details.get('bac', '?'))}%) <<<")
                    elif result.get("success"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> SUKCES: +{r2(details.get('heal_amount', 0))} HEAL — cel: {r2(details.get('target_con_after', '?'))} CON <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO (rzut {details.get('roll', '?')} vs BAC {r2(details.get('bac', '?'))}%) <<<")
                
                # --- RES buffy/debuffy ---
                elif isinstance(ability_type, str) and (ability_type.startswith("RES_") or ability_type == "RES_TEMP"):
                    if "roll" in details:
                        crit = " (KRYTYK!)" if details.get("is_critical") else ""
                        log_to_audit(f"  Rzut: {details.get('roll', '?')}{crit}")
                    if result.get("success"):
                        val = r2(details.get("value", "?"))
                        dur = details.get("duration", "?")
                        dom = details.get("domain_affected")
                        scope = f"vs {dom}" if dom else "profil"
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> SUKCES: RES {scope} ({val}) na {dur} aktywacji <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")
                
                # --- COOLDOWN ---
                elif ability_type in ["COOLDOWN_BURN", "COOLDOWN_REFRESH"]:
                    if result.get("success"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> SUKCES: CD {details.get('affected_slots', 'brak')} <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")
                
                # --- AOE ---
                elif ability_type == "AOE":
                    hit_results = details.get("hit_results", [])
                    aoe_sub = details.get("aoe_type", "DD")
                    log_to_audit(f"  AOE {aoe_sub} (zasięg efektu: {details.get('effect_range', '?')})")
                    
                    for hr in hit_results:
                        eff = hr.get("effect", "DD")
                        if eff == "DD":
                            if hr.get("bac_success"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: TRAFIENIE {r2(hr['damage_dealt'])} DMG — {r2(hr['target_con_after'])} CON")
                            elif hr.get("dva_success"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DVA trafienie, BAC pudło")
                            else:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: PUDŁO")
                        elif eff == "DOT":
                            if hr.get("dot_applied"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DOT {r2(hr.get('tick', '?'))}/akt x{hr.get('duration', '?')}")
                            else:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: PUDŁO")
                        elif eff == "HEAL":
                            if hr.get("bac_success"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: +{r2(hr.get('heal_amount', 0))} HEAL — {r2(hr['target_con_after'])} CON")
                            else:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: HEAL pudło")
                        elif eff == "HOT":
                            log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: HOT {r2(hr.get('tick', '?'))}/akt x{hr.get('duration', '?')}")
                        elif eff in ["RES_BUFF", "RES_DEBUFF"]:
                            if hr.get("res_applied"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: {hr.get('res_mode', '?')} {r2(hr.get('res_value', '?'))} x{hr.get('res_duration', '?')}")
                            else:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: PUDŁO")
                        elif eff in ["CHARM", "FEAR"]:
                            if hr.get("status_applied"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: {eff} na {hr.get('duration', '?')} aktywacji")
                            elif hr.get("dva_success"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DVA trafienie, BAC pudło")
                            else:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: PUDŁO")
                        elif eff == "DRAINLIFE":
                            if hr.get("bac_success"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DRAIN {r2(hr['damage_dealt'])} DMG — {r2(hr['target_con_after'])} CON")
                            elif hr.get("dva_success"):
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DVA trafienie, BAC pudło")
                            else:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: PUDŁO")
                        elif eff == "TEMP_HP":
                            log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: +{r2(hr.get('temp_hp_gained', 0))} TEMP_HP")
                        elif eff == "CLEANSE":
                            if hr.get("bac_success"):
                                removed = hr.get("removed", [])
                                if removed:
                                    log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: CLEANSE usunięto {len(removed)}: {', '.join(removed)}")
                                else:
                                    log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: CLEANSE — brak efektów do usunięcia")
                            elif hr.get("bac_success") is False:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: CLEANSE pudło (BAC {hr.get('bac_roll','?')} vs {r2(details.get('bac','?'))}%)")
                        elif eff == "DISPEL":
                            if hr.get("bac_success"):
                                removed = hr.get("removed", [])
                                if removed:
                                    log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DISPEL usunięto {len(removed)}: {', '.join(removed)}")
                                else:
                                    log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DISPEL — brak efektów do usunięcia")
                            elif hr.get("bac_success") is False:
                                log_to_audit(f"  -> {hr['name']}: DISPEL pudło (BAC {hr.get('bac_roll','?')} vs {r2(details.get('bac','?'))}%)")
                        if hr.get("eliminated"):
                            log_to_audit(f"     >>> {hr['name']} WYELIMINOWANY <<<")
                    
                    if aoe_sub == "DRAINLIFE" and details.get("total_healed"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  DRAIN HEAL: +{r2(details['total_healed'])} — {unit['name']}: {r2(details.get('attacker_con_after', '?'))} CON")
                
                # --- SETUP ---
                elif ability_type == "SETUP":
                    if result.get("success"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> SETUP: {details.get('stacks_before', '?')} → {details.get('stacks_after', '?')}/3 <<<")

                # --- TEMP_HP ---
                elif ability_type == "TEMP_HP":
                    if result.get("success"):
                        dur_txt = f" na {details.get('duration')} akt" if details.get("duration") else " (stały)"
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> TEMP_HP: +{r2(details.get('temp_hp_gained', 0))}{dur_txt} — cel: {r2(details.get('target_con_after', '?'))} CON <<<")

                # --- REFLECT ---
                elif ability_type == "REFLECT":
                    if result.get("success"):
                        slots = details.get("slots", "?")
                        bac = r2(details.get("bac_per_slot", "?"))
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> REFLECT: {slots} slotów ({bac}% BAC każdy) na {target_name} <<<")

                # --- CLEANSE ---
                elif ability_type == "CLEANSE":
                    if result.get("success"):
                        removed = details.get("removed", [])
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> CLEANSE: usunięto {len(removed)} efektów: {', '.join(removed)} <<<")

                # --- DISPEL ---
                elif ability_type == "DISPEL":
                    if result.get("success"):
                        removed = details.get("removed", [])
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> DISPEL: usunięto {len(removed)} efektów: {', '.join(removed)} <<<")

                # --- Fallback ---
                else:
                    if result.get("success"):
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> SUKCES <<<")
                    else:
                        log_to_audit(f"  >>> PUDŁO <<<")
                
                # --- Eliminacja (single target) ---
                if details.get("target_eliminated"):
                    log_to_audit(f"  >>> {target_name} WYELIMINOWANY <<<")
                
               
                # --- Empowered reset ---
                if details.get("empowered_reset"):
                    log_to_audit(f"  EMPOWERED: setup stacks zresetowane (3 → 0)")
                
                # --- Renoma ---
